# Install Link (PR 1) — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** One shared, switchable install link per organisation. A technician opens it on the machine, the agent installs itself, the machine enrols into *waiting for review* where it can receive only inventory, health monitoring and certificate lifecycle jobs, and a technician approves it or rejects it (which uninstalls it).

**Architecture:** The link is a kernel enrollment credential: table `install_links` (RLS, one row per org, code stored as SHA-256), service `app/services/install_link.py`, admin routes `/api/v1/install-link` (`enrollment.link.manage`), and an additive `link_code` alternative on `POST /enroll/v1`. `devices.review_state` plus a gate inside `JobEngine.create` (the one place every job is born) holds pending devices back; automatic dispatchers pass over them so a pending device cannot stall a sweep. The public page `/i/<code>`, the Windows download redirect and `install.sh` are server-rendered by the `agent_distribution` module and routed to `api` by Traefik. Agent 1.6.0 gains a Windows self-installer (`agent/internal/install`, entered only when the binary's own file name carries a code) and a signed `agent.uninstall` capability. The console gains an *Install link* panel on Agent packages and a *Waiting for review* tab, row actions and nav count on Devices.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12 · FastAPI · SQLAlchemy 2.x (typed) · Pydantic v2 · Alembic · Celery beat · Go 1.24 (`golang.org/x/sys` v0.33.0: `windows`, `windows/svc/mgr`, `windows/registry`) · React + TypeScript (strict) · TanStack Query · Vitest.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-install-link-and-windows-push-design.md` — §0, §1 (1.1–1.6), §3 (the two codes below), §4 (link_code, `agent.uninstall`, stable ≥ 1.6.0), §5 Part 1, §6 item 1. **Part 2 (WinRM push) is NOT in this plan.**

## Global Constraints

- **CLAUDE.md invariants hold unchanged.** 1: every job (including `agent.uninstall`) is created by `JobEngine.create`, signed, and verified by the agent; the link path issues a certificate and dispatches no job. 2: the link-installed agent connects outbound over mTLS; the control plane opens no connection; the agent opens no listening port. 3: nothing here stores a credential; the link code is hashed like an enrollment token. 4: link on/off, settings changes, regeneration, every link enrollment, approve, reject and rejection completion are audited. 5: `install_links` carries `org_id` under ENABLE + FORCE RLS; every query also carries an explicit `org_id` predicate. 6: `enrollment.link.manage` and `devices.review` are checked server-side; the console only hides.
- **Link code:** 32 characters from the URL-safe alphabet `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, generated by `secrets.token_urlsafe(24)` (192 bits, ≥ 160). Stored **only** as `sha256(code)` hex in `install_links.code_hash`; the full link is returned **only** in the response that minted the code (first switch-on or regenerate).
- **Link URL:** `https://<console>/i/<code>`. Console base = `ITS_PUBLIC_CONSOLE_URL` when set, else `https://<Host header>`.
- **Hourly limit:** default **50** link enrollments per org per rolling hour, configurable **1–500**. Over the limit: **429**, audited **once per hour** (`enrollment.link_rate_limited`).
- **Networks:** CIDRs (IPv4 and IPv6), host bits zero, at most 64. **At least one network is required to switch the link on** — enforced by the API (422) and by a CHECK constraint.
- **404 is indistinguishable** for link off / past its switch-off time / wrong code / source outside every network / malformed code / unknown arch: body exactly `{"detail":"Not Found"}` (FastAPI's own 404 body), same headers, on the page, the download, `install.sh` and `POST /enroll/v1`.
- **Source address:** the **rightmost** `X-Forwarded-For` entry, honoured **only** when the direct peer is inside `ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS` (default `172.19.240.0/24`, the compose network Traefik sits on — `ITMS_NETWORK_SUBNET`); from any other peer the header is ignored and the peer address is the source. A rightmost entry that does not parse is "no address" (404).
- **Windows file name:** `ITMS-Agent-Setup_<host>[@<port>]_<code>.exe` (D6a; first drafted as `ITMS-Agent-Setup_<code>.exe`), served as `Content-Disposition` signed into a **15-minute** presigned URL (`DOWNLOAD_TTL_S = 900`). The agent tolerates a browser's ` (n)` / `(n)` suffix and any case of `.exe`. The bytes are the published artefact, untouched.
- **The code is never an argument** (it *is* the Windows installer's argv[0] while it runs — D11). The Windows self-installer reads it from its own file name; the elevated re-launch carries only `--elevated-install`; the service binPath carries no arguments; `install.sh` pipes it through the shell builtin `printf` into `install.sh --token-stdin`.
- **Allow-list while pending** (exactly): `inventory.collect`, `health.policy.apply`, `health.check.now`, `agent.cert.renew`, `agent.cert.install`, `agent.uninstall`. **While rejected:** `agent.uninstall` only. Anything else is refused **before signing** with **409 `"waiting for review"`**, and the body and exception name the device and the job type. Promoting a pending device to site server: **409**.
- **Agent version:** the link offers only a **stable** release **≥ 1.6.0** (`LINK_MIN_AGENT_VERSION`). The agent side ships as release **1.6.0**.
- **Permission codes (new):** `enrollment.link.manage` — admin tier (`super-admin`, `org-admin`); `devices.review` — tech tier (`super-admin`, `org-admin`, `technician`). Neither is a `PLATFORM_OPERATOR_CODE`. `agent.uninstall` is in `NON_APPROVABLE_REASONS` (no automated or AI actor).
- **Audit actions (exact):** `enrollment.link_created`, `enrollment.link_enabled`, `enrollment.link_disabled` (metadata `reason`: `operator` | `scheduled`), `enrollment.link_settings_changed`, `enrollment.link_regenerated`, `enrollment.link_rate_limited`, `agent.enrolled_via_link` (spec name; metadata carries `source_address`), `device.review_approved`, `device.review_rejected`, `device.rejection_completed`. No audit metadata ever contains a code.
- **Notification event (exact):** `device.joined_via_link`.
- **Migrations are forward-only.** New revision **`0170`**, `down_revision = "0160"`. Its `downgrade()` is written to run (CI runs `upgrade head → downgrade base → upgrade head`). No new grants file entry is needed: `init-app-user.sh`'s default privileges cover new tables.
- **No new dependencies** in the backend, the frontend or the agent. The agent reuses `golang.org/x/sys` v0.33.0 (already required): `windows.ShellExecute`, `windows.MessageBox`, `windows.GetCurrentProcessToken().IsElevated()`, `windows.MoveFileEx`, `svc/mgr`, `registry`; the data-directory ACL reuses `internal/secdir.Restrict` (the same `D:PAI(A;OICI;FA;;;SY)(A;OICI;FA;;;BA)` install.ps1 sets).
- **Idle budget:** `internal/install` has no `init()` and no package-level variable initialised by a call; the service path never calls into it. The uninstall capability registers as a map insert and does nothing until a verified job arrives.
- **`backend/app/modules/agent_distribution/**` must not contain** the substrings `paramiko`, `asyncssh`, `fabric`, `pywinrm`, `winrm`, `smbprotocol`, `impacket`, `pypsrp`, `socket.create_connection`, `asyncio.open_connection`, `subprocess` — anywhere, including docstrings (`tests/test_agent_distribution_opens_no_connection.py` is a text match).
- **Frontend:** strict TypeScript, no `any`; no wire name on screen (job types, permission codes and `review_state` values render through label maps); every `ApiClient` method a routed page calls has a fixture (harmony `--strict-fixtures`).
- **Verification commands** (run the relevant ones at the end of every task; all of them before the final commit):
  - backend, from `backend/`: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q`, `.venv/bin/ruff check app tests`, `.venv/bin/mypy app`
  - frontend, from `frontend/`: `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx vitest run`, `npm run build`, `node scripts/harmony.mjs --routes /agent-packages,/devices --strict-fixtures`
  - agent, from `agent/`: `go test ./...`, `go vet ./...`, `staticcheck ./...`, `GOOS=windows go vet ./...`, `GOOS=darwin go vet ./...`

## Decisions this plan takes where the spec is silent or disagrees with the code

These are recorded here so a reviewer can reject one without re-reading the whole plan.

- **D1 — "Remove the device row" is a tombstone, not a DELETE.** No device-delete path exists anywhere in the platform; ~35 foreign keys reference `devices.id` with no `ON DELETE`, and `security_events` (which a pending machine can populate — the agent ships security events on its own cadence) is append-only to `app_user`. Reject therefore sets `review_state='rejected'`, and completion (agent confirmed, or 24 h) sets `status='decommissioned'` and revokes the certificate. Rejected rows are excluded from every list, tab and count. `review_state` gains a third value, `rejected`.
- **D2 — Revocation happens at completion, not at the Reject click.** Revoking first would drop the live connection (`publish_revoke`) and make `agent.uninstall` undeliverable. An agent that does not advertise `agent.uninstall` (older than 1.6.0) is revoked and tombstoned immediately, and the response tells the technician to remove the agent by hand (spec §4).
- **D3 — The link path never auto-links to an existing asset row.** `resolve_enrolment_target` links on agent-reported MACs; a leaked code plus spoofed MACs would otherwise land a pending agent on an operator-vouched row, and rejecting it would tombstone the real asset. Link enrolments always create a fresh row.
- **D4 — Automatic dispatchers pass over non-approved devices.** The gate raises; a sweep that hits it would abort its whole org cycle (e.g. `logs.ship` and `patch.scan` sweeps select every online agent). Task 5 adds `review_state = 'approved'` to each automatic selection that can reach a pending device, with a test per site. The gate stays the guarantee; the predicates keep sweeps alive.
- **D5 — Install paths are install.ps1's, not the spec's.** The spec writes `C:\Program Files\ITS Agent\its-agent.exe`; the service, `uninstall.ps1`, self-update and `config.defaultDataDir` all use `%ProgramFiles%\ITSuite\its-agent.exe` and `%ProgramData%\ITSuite\agent`. The self-installer uses those, so there is exactly one install layout.
- **D6 — The Windows self-installer learns the console URL from the download's Mark of the Web.** The file name carries only the code and the bytes are shared by every tenant, so the enroll URL must come from somewhere else: the `ReferrerUrl` in `<exe>:Zone.Identifier`, reduced to its `https` origin. The page sends `Referrer-Policy: origin`, which also keeps the code out of the object store's request logs. With no usable Mark of the Web the installer refuses with a plain message ("download it again with Edge or Chrome from the install link") rather than guessing.
  - **Superseded by the operator, 2026-09-28 — D6a:** the console is carried in the file name instead, `ITMS-Agent-Setup_<host>[@<port>]_<code>.exe`, parsed from the right (the code is the last 32 characters after a `_`; the host is a validated DNS name, no IP literals, no underscores; port 1–65535; the URL is `https://<host>[:<port>]` with normal TLS verification). So a copy from a share or USB stick installs too. `motw.go` is not written. The Windows download (Task 7) builds that name from the hardened console base, and answers 404 when the console host is not a DNS name. `Referrer-Policy: origin` stays, for its other purpose: keeping the code out of the object store's request logs. Also decided the same day: `job.cancel` joins the pending allow-list (cancelling only reduces what runs), and pending machines stay out of self-update rollouts until approved (Task 5 as written).
- **D7 — `agent.uninstall` reports `scheduled`, then a detached helper removes everything.** A process cannot delete its own running service and then report; the capability returns `{"scheduled": true}` after launching a helper that waits 15 s (so the result reaches the gateway), stops and deletes the service, removes binary, config and state, and exits. The server treats a `succeeded` `agent.uninstall` as the confirmation.
- **D8 — The link code rides in the Windows download's presigned URL** (`response-content-disposition` is signed into it), so it can appear in object-store access logs if the operator enables them. Documented as a residual in the packaging README amendment (Task 17); bounded by §1.6.
- **D9 — Install-link push-target hardening.** `agent_push.preflight._asset_for` additionally requires `review_state='approved'`: a pending row was vouched for by nobody, and 2a(d) is about a person vouching.
- **D10 — "Agent 1.6.0" is a release, not a source edit.** No version constant exists in the tree: the agent's version is stamped at build time from `release-agent.yml`'s input. The floors are Task 7's `LINK_MIN_AGENT_VERSION` (the page offers only stable ≥ 1.6.0) and Task 9's capability check (`agent.uninstall` is sent only to an agent that advertised it). Task 18's Appendix B cuts 1.6.0 after the merge.
- **D11 — The link code is in the installer's argv[0], and nowhere else in any command line.** The downloaded file is named with the code, so the installer process's image path contains it while it runs (and the file stays in Downloads, spec §0 item 1). It is never an argument: the UAC re-launch passes only `--elevated-install`, the service is registered with no arguments, and its environment holds the URL and data directory only. Because it is the image path, process-creation logging records it: Security event 4688 (with command-line auditing), Sysmon event 1, and whatever an EDR or SIEM forwards off the machine (review I8). Recorded in the packaging README (Task 17).

### Task 1: Trusted proxy setting and the request's source address

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/app/core/config.py` (after `public_console_url`, ~line 134)
- Create: `backend/app/core/client_address.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/main.py` (`create_app`, right after `app.state.settings = settings`)
- Modify: `deploy/docker-compose.yml` (`services.api.environment`)
- Modify: `deploy/.env.example` (new section after `--- Console address ... ---`)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_client_address.py`, `backend/tests/test_deploy_install_link.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `IPAddress`, `IPNetwork` type aliases; `parse_trusted_proxies(value: str) -> tuple[IPNetwork, ...]` (raises `TrustedProxyConfigError(ValueError)`); `client_address(request: Request, trusted: Sequence[IPNetwork]) -> IPAddress | None`; `address_in(address: IPAddress | None, networks: Iterable[object]) -> bool`; `app.state.trusted_proxies: tuple[IPNetwork, ...]`; `Settings.trusted_proxy_cidrs: str`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`backend/tests/test_client_address.py`:

```python
"""The source address of a request (install-link spec §1.1).

The header is honoured only from a peer inside ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS, and
then only its RIGHTMOST entry -- the one Traefik wrote. Everything to the left
of it is whatever the client sent.
"""

import ipaddress

import pytest
from starlette.requests import Request

from app.core.client_address import (
    TrustedProxyConfigError,
    address_in,
    client_address,
    parse_trusted_proxies,
)

TRUSTED = parse_trusted_proxies("172.19.240.0/24")


def _request(peer: str | None, *xff: str) -> Request:
    scope = {
        "type": "http",
        "method": "GET",
        "path": "/",
        "headers": [(b"x-forwarded-for", value.encode()) for value in xff],
        "client": (peer, 5000) if peer is not None else None,
    }
    return Request(scope)


def test_a_peer_outside_the_trusted_set_is_the_source_and_its_header_is_ignored() -> None:
    got = client_address(_request("198.51.100.4", "10.50.0.9"), TRUSTED)
    assert got == ipaddress.ip_address("198.51.100.4")


def test_from_traefik_the_rightmost_entry_is_the_source() -> None:
    # The client claimed 10.50.0.9; Traefik appended the real 203.0.113.7.
    got = client_address(_request("172.19.240.3", "10.50.0.9, 203.0.113.7"), TRUSTED)
    assert got == ipaddress.ip_address("203.0.113.7")


def test_two_header_lines_are_one_list_and_the_last_entry_wins() -> None:
    got = client_address(_request("172.19.240.3", "10.50.0.9", "192.0.2.10"), TRUSTED)
    assert got == ipaddress.ip_address("192.0.2.10")


def test_a_trusted_peer_with_no_header_is_itself() -> None:
    assert client_address(_request("172.19.240.3"), TRUSTED) == ipaddress.ip_address(
        "172.19.240.3"
    )


def test_an_unparseable_rightmost_entry_is_no_address_at_all() -> None:
    assert client_address(_request("172.19.240.3", "10.0.0.1, not-an-ip"), TRUSTED) is None


def test_an_ipv4_mapped_peer_is_compared_as_ipv4() -> None:
    got = client_address(_request("::ffff:172.19.240.3", "10.50.0.9"), TRUSTED)
    assert got == ipaddress.ip_address("10.50.0.9")


def test_no_client_is_no_address() -> None:
    assert client_address(_request(None), TRUSTED) is None


def test_the_setting_refuses_a_prefix_with_host_bits() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TrustedProxyConfigError):
        parse_trusted_proxies("172.19.240.1/24")


def test_the_setting_takes_several_prefixes_and_ignores_blanks() -> None:
    nets = parse_trusted_proxies(" 10.0.0.0/8, ,fd00::/8 ")
    assert [str(n) for n in nets] == ["10.0.0.0/8", "fd00::/8"]


def test_containment_across_versions_is_false_not_an_error() -> None:
    v6 = ipaddress.ip_address("fd00::1")
    assert address_in(v6, ["10.0.0.0/8"]) is False
    assert address_in(v6, ["fd00::/8"]) is True
    assert address_in(None, ["0.0.0.0/0"]) is False
```

`backend/tests/test_deploy_install_link.py` (grows in Task 7):

```python
"""Deployment facts the install link depends on, asserted against the files."""

from pathlib import Path

import yaml

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
COMPOSE = REPO / "deploy" / "docker-compose.yml"
ENV_EXAMPLE = REPO / "deploy" / ".env.example"


def _api() -> dict:
    return yaml.safe_load(COMPOSE.read_text())["services"]["api"]


def test_the_api_trusts_the_compose_network_by_default() -> None:
    value = _api()["environment"]["ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS"]
    assert value == "${ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS:-${ITMS_NETWORK_SUBNET:-172.19.240.0/24}}"


def test_uvicorn_is_not_told_to_rewrite_the_client_address() -> None:
    # If uvicorn trusted Traefik's header itself, request.client would already
    # be the LEFTMOST (client-supplied) entry and the check in
    # app/core/client_address.py would be comparing against a forgery.
    api = _api()
    assert "--forwarded-allow-ips" not in api["command"]
    assert "--proxy-headers" not in api["command"]
    assert "FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS" not in api["environment"]


def test_the_setting_is_documented_where_the_operator_edits() -> None:
    text = ENV_EXAMPLE.read_text()
    assert "--- Trusted proxy (install link) ---" in text
    assert "ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS" in text
```

`backend/app/core/config.py`, add after `public_console_url: str | None = None`:

```python
    # The ONLY peers whose X-Forwarded-For header this API believes
    # (install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.1). Comma-separated network prefixes.
    # The default is the compose network Traefik sits on (ITMS_NETWORK_SUBNET
    # in deploy/.env.example); from any other peer the header is ignored and
    # the TCP peer is the source address. Parsed and validated at startup by
    # app/core/client_address.py so a typo fails the process, not a request.
    trusted_proxy_cidrs: str = "172.19.240.0/24"
```

`backend/app/core/client_address.py`:

```python
"""Where a request came from, for decisions that depend on it.

Install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.1. The link answers only requests from networks
an operator listed, so the source address is a security input, and the API sits
behind Traefik: the TCP peer is Traefik, and the client's address is in
`X-Forwarded-For`.

THE HEADER IS BELIEVED ONLY FROM A TRUSTED PEER, AND ONLY ITS RIGHTMOST ENTRY.
Traefik APPENDS the address it saw; everything to the left of that is whatever
the client sent, so a client that writes `X-Forwarded-For: 10.50.0.9` into its
own request cannot claim an allowed network -- its claim is not the rightmost
entry by the time the request arrives. From a peer outside
`ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS` the header is ignored entirely: anybody who can reach
the API port directly could otherwise write it.

UVICORN MUST NOT REWRITE `request.client` ITSELF. It would take the LEFTMOST
entry. `tests/test_deploy_install_link.py` pins that the compose command does not
pass `--forwarded-allow-ips` / `--proxy-headers`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
from collections.abc import Iterable, Sequence

from starlette.requests import Request

IPAddress = ipaddress.IPv4Address | ipaddress.IPv6Address
IPNetwork = ipaddress.IPv4Network | ipaddress.IPv6Network


class TrustedProxyConfigError(ValueError):
    """`ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS` holds something that is not a network prefix."""


def parse_trusted_proxies(value: str) -> tuple[IPNetwork, ...]:
    """The configured prefixes, strictly: a prefix with host bits set is refused,
    because `172.19.240.1/24` is far more often a typo than an intention."""
    networks: list[IPNetwork] = []
    for raw in value.split(","):
        item = raw.strip()
        if not item:
            continue
        try:
            networks.append(ipaddress.ip_network(item, strict=True))
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise TrustedProxyConfigError(
                f"ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS: {item!r} is not a network prefix ({exc})"
            ) from exc
    return tuple(networks)


def _address(value: str) -> IPAddress | None:
    try:
        parsed = ipaddress.ip_address(value.strip())
    except ValueError:
        return None
    if isinstance(parsed, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and parsed.ipv4_mapped is not None:
        return parsed.ipv4_mapped
    return parsed


def address_in(address: IPAddress | None, networks: Iterable[object]) -> bool:
    """Is `address` inside any of `networks`? Accepts `ip_network` objects or
    their strings (asyncpg hands `cidr[]` back as objects, the API as strings).
    No address is inside nothing; a v6 address is never inside a v4 prefix."""
    if address is None:
        return False
    for raw in networks:
        network = ipaddress.ip_network(str(raw), strict=False)
        if network.version == address.version and address in network:
            return True
    return False


def client_address(request: Request, trusted: Sequence[IPNetwork]) -> IPAddress | None:
    """The request's source address, or `None` when it cannot be established.

    `None` is a refusal at every caller: the install link answers it with the
    same 404 as a link that does not exist.
    """
    if request.client is None:
        return None
    peer = _address(request.client.host)
    if peer is None or not address_in(peer, trusted):
        return peer
    forwarded = ",".join(request.headers.getlist("x-forwarded-for"))
    entries = [entry.strip() for entry in forwarded.split(",") if entry.strip()]
    if not entries:
        return peer
    return _address(entries[-1])
```

```python
    # startup instead of 500ing the first install-link request.
    from app.core.client_address import parse_trusted_proxies

    app.state.trusted_proxies = parse_trusted_proxies(settings.trusted_proxy_cidrs)
```

```sh
# --- Trusted proxy (install link) ---
# The only peers whose X-Forwarded-For header the API believes when it decides
# whether an install-link request comes from a network you allowed. Leave it
# unset: docker-compose.yml defaults it to ITMS_NETWORK_SUBNET, the network
# Traefik sits on. Set it only if a proxy you run in front of ITMS forwards to
# Traefik from somewhere else. Comma-separated prefixes, host bits zero.
```

```python
"""Migration 0170: install_links, devices.review_state, and two permission codes."""

from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import select, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession

from app.core.tenancy import org_session
from app.models import Device, InstallLink, Organization


async def _org(admin_engine: AsyncEngine) -> Organization:
    slug = f"il-{uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:
        org = Organization(name=slug, slug=slug)
        session.add(org)
        await session.commit()
        return org


async def test_install_links_is_forced_rls_with_a_tenant_policy(admin_engine: AsyncEngine) -> None:
    async with admin_engine.connect() as conn:
        row = (
            await conn.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT relrowsecurity, relforcerowsecurity FROM pg_class "
                    "WHERE relname = 'install_links'"
                )
            )
        ).one()
        policies = (
            await conn.execute(
                text("SELECT policyname FROM pg_policies WHERE tablename = 'install_links'")
            )
        ).scalars().all()
    assert tuple(row) == (True, True)
    assert policies == ["tenant_isolation"]


async def test_one_org_never_reads_another_orgs_link(
    admin_engine: AsyncEngine, app_engine: AsyncEngine
) -> None:
    a, b = await _org(admin_engine), await _org(admin_engine)
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        session.add_all([InstallLink(org_id=a.id), InstallLink(org_id=b.id)])
        await session.commit()
    async with org_session(b.id, engine=app_engine) as session:
        seen = (await session.execute(select(InstallLink.org_id))).scalars().all()
    assert seen == [b.id]


async def test_the_link_cannot_be_on_without_a_network(admin_engine: AsyncEngine) -> None:
    org = await _org(admin_engine)

        session.add(InstallLink(org_id=org.id, enabled=True, code_hash="a" * 64, networks=[]))
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="ck_install_links_on_needs_code_and_network"):
            await session.commit()


async def test_the_hourly_limit_is_bounded(admin_engine: AsyncEngine) -> None:
    org = await _org(admin_engine)
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        session.add(InstallLink(org_id=org.id, hourly_limit=501))
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="ck_install_links_hourly_limit"):

) -> None:

        session.add(device)
        await session.commit()

    assert device.review_state == "approved"
    assert device.link_joined_from is None and device.rejected_at is None


async def test_review_state_is_a_closed_vocabulary(admin_engine: AsyncEngine) -> None:
    org = await _org(admin_engine)
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        session.add(Device(org_id=org.id, hostname="h", review_state="maybe"))
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="ck_devices_review_state"):

    ("role", "expected"),
    [
        ("super-admin", {"enrollment.link.manage", "devices.review"}),
        ("org-admin", {"enrollment.link.manage", "devices.review"}),
        ("technician", {"devices.review"}),
        ("auditor", set()),
        ("read-only", set()),
    ],
)
async def test_the_codes_are_seeded_to_the_tiers_the_spec_names(
    admin_engine: AsyncEngine, role: str, expected: set[str]
) -> None:
    async with admin_engine.connect() as conn:
        held = set(
            (
                await conn.execute(
                    text(
                        "SELECT p.code FROM permissions p "
                        "JOIN role_permissions rp ON rp.permission_id = p.id "
                        "JOIN roles r ON r.id = rp.role_id "
                        "WHERE r.name = :role AND r.org_id IS NULL "
                        "AND p.code IN ('enrollment.link.manage', 'devices.review')"
                    ),
                    {"role": role},
                )
            ).scalars()
        )
    assert held == expected
```

`backend/migrations/versions/0170_install_link.py`:

```python
"""install link: one switchable enrollment link per org, and devices waiting for review

docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-install-link-and-windows-push-design.md §1.1,
§1.5 and §3.

`install_links` -- ONE ROW PER ORG (a unique `org_id`), because the operator
asked for one link for all devices. The code is stored ONLY as its SHA-256
(`code_hash`, unique across orgs, because the public endpoints resolve the org
FROM the hash exactly as `/enroll/v1` resolves it from a token id). `networks`
is native `cidr[]` for the reason migrations 0043/0050/0077 give: the only
question asked of it is containment.

THE CHECK CONSTRAINT RESTATES THE API'S RULE: a link that is on has a code and
at least one network (spec §1.1: "an empty list is not 'anywhere'"). The API
refuses first with a sentence; this is the floor under a future caller that
forgets.

`devices.review_state` -- `approved` for every existing row and every token
enrollment; `pending` for a link enrollment; `rejected` once a technician
rejects it (plan D1: a tombstone, because no device-delete path exists and
`security_events` is append-only). `link_joined_from`/`link_joined_at` say how,
from where and when a machine joined, and the rate limit counts
`link_joined_at` inside the last hour.

Two permission codes: `enrollment.link.manage` (admin tier) and
`devices.review` (technician tier). Neither is a platform-operator code: both
act on org-scoped, RLS-protected rows.

Revision ID: 0170
Revises: 0160
"""

from collections.abc import Sequence

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

revision: str = "0170"
down_revision: str | None = "0160"
branch_labels: str | Sequence[str] | None = None
depends_on: str | Sequence[str] | None = None

_ORG_GUC = "current_setting('app.current_org', true)::uuid"

PERMISSIONS = [
    ("enrollment.link.manage", "enrollment", "link.manage"),
    ("devices.review", "devices", "review"),
]
ROLE_GRANTS: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    "super-admin": ["enrollment.link.manage", "devices.review"],
    "org-admin": ["enrollment.link.manage", "devices.review"],
    "technician": ["devices.review"],
}


def upgrade() -> None:
    op.create_table(
        "install_links",
        sa.Column("id", postgresql.UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column(
            "org_id",
            postgresql.UUID(as_uuid=True),
            sa.ForeignKey("organizations.id"),
            nullable=False,
        ),
        sa.Column("enabled", sa.Boolean(), server_default=sa.false(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("code_hash", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.Column(
            "networks",
            postgresql.ARRAY(postgresql.CIDR()),
            server_default=sa.text("'{}'::cidr[]"),
            nullable=False,
        ),
        sa.Column("disable_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("hourly_limit", sa.Integer(), server_default=sa.text("50"), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("enabled_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("rate_limited_audit_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True),
        sa.Column(
            "created_by", postgresql.UUID(as_uuid=True), sa.ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True
        ),
        sa.Column(
            "created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False
        ),
        sa.Column(
            "updated_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False
        ),
        sa.UniqueConstraint("org_id", name="uq_install_links_org"),
        sa.UniqueConstraint("code_hash", name="uq_install_links_code_hash"),
        sa.CheckConstraint("hourly_limit BETWEEN 1 AND 500", name="ck_install_links_hourly_limit"),
        sa.CheckConstraint("cardinality(networks) <= 64", name="ck_install_links_network_count"),
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "NOT enabled OR (code_hash IS NOT NULL AND cardinality(networks) >= 1)",
            name="ck_install_links_on_needs_code_and_network",
        ),
    )
    op.execute("ALTER TABLE install_links ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
    op.execute("ALTER TABLE install_links FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
    op.execute(
        "CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation ON install_links "
        f"USING (org_id = {_ORG_GUC}) WITH CHECK (org_id = {_ORG_GUC})"
    )

    op.add_column(
        "devices",
        sa.Column("review_state", sa.String(10), server_default="approved", nullable=False),
    )
    op.create_check_constraint(
        "ck_devices_review_state",
        "devices",
        "review_state IN ('approved', 'pending', 'rejected')",
    )
    op.add_column("devices", sa.Column("link_joined_from", postgresql.INET(), nullable=True))
    op.add_column(
        "devices", sa.Column("link_joined_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    )
    op.add_column("devices", sa.Column("rejected_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True))
    op.create_index("ix_devices_org_review_state", "devices", ["org_id", "review_state"])

    for code, module, action in PERMISSIONS:
        op.execute(
            "INSERT INTO permissions (id, code, module, action) "
            f"VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), '{code}', '{module}', '{action}')"
        )
    for role, codes in ROLE_GRANTS.items():
        code_list = ", ".join(f"'{c}'" for c in codes)
        op.execute(
            "INSERT INTO role_permissions (role_id, permission_id) "
            "SELECT r.id, p.id FROM roles r, permissions p "
            f"WHERE r.name = '{role}' AND r.org_id IS NULL AND p.code IN ({code_list})"
        )


def downgrade() -> None:
    # BY CODE, never by module: `module = 'devices'` and `module = 'enrollment'`
    # hold codes from migration 0003 that must survive the way down.
    code_list = ", ".join(f"'{c}'" for c, _, _ in PERMISSIONS)
    op.execute(
        "DELETE FROM role_permissions WHERE permission_id IN "
        f"(SELECT id FROM permissions WHERE code IN ({code_list}))"
    )
    op.execute(f"DELETE FROM permissions WHERE code IN ({code_list})")
    op.drop_index("ix_devices_org_review_state", table_name="devices")
    op.drop_column("devices", "rejected_at")
    op.drop_column("devices", "link_joined_at")
    op.drop_column("devices", "link_joined_from")
    op.drop_constraint("ck_devices_review_state", "devices", type_="check")
    op.drop_column("devices", "review_state")
    op.execute("DROP POLICY IF EXISTS tenant_isolation ON install_links")
    op.drop_table("install_links")
```

```python
class InstallLink(Base):
    """One organisation's install link (install-link spec §1.1).

    THE CODE IS NOT HERE. `code_hash` is `sha256(code)` hex; the code itself is
    returned once, by the response that minted it, exactly as an enrollment
    token is. Unique across orgs because the public page and `/enroll/v1`
    resolve the organisation FROM the hash.
    """

    __tablename__ = "install_links"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("org_id", name="uq_install_links_org"),
        UniqueConstraint("code_hash", name="uq_install_links_code_hash"),
    )

    id: Mapped[UUID] = uuid7_pk()
    org_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("organizations.id"))
    enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))
    code_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), default=None)
    networks: Mapped[list[str]] = mapped_column(
        ARRAY(CIDR()), default=list, server_default=text("'{}'::cidr[]")
    )
    disable_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(default=None)
    hourly_limit: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(default=50, server_default=text("50"))
    #: When the link was last switched on; NULL while it is off. "How long it
    #: has been on" and "how many joined since" are both measured from here.
    enabled_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(default=None)
    #: The last `enrollment.link_rate_limited` audit row, so a flood of 429s
    #: writes one row an hour rather than one per request.
    rate_limited_audit_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(default=None)
    created_by: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"), default=None)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(server_default=func.now())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())
```

- Produces (all in `app.services.install_link`): `CODE_LENGTH = 32`, `DEFAULT_HOURLY_LIMIT = 50`, `MIN_HOURLY_LIMIT = 1`, `MAX_HOURLY_LIMIT = 500`, `MAX_NETWORKS = 64`, `RATE_WINDOW: timedelta`; `InstallLinkError(ValueError)`; `new_code() -> str`; `hash_code(code: str) -> str`; `looks_like_code(value: str) -> bool`; `normalise_networks(values: list[str]) -> list[str]`; `is_live(link: InstallLink, now: datetime) -> bool`; `LinkView` dataclass; `view(session, org_id, now) -> LinkView`; `save(session, *, org_id, actor_id, enabled, networks, disable_at, hourly_limit, now) -> str | None` (returns a newly minted code or `None`); `regenerate(session, *, org_id, actor_id, now) -> str`; `resolve_org(admin_engine, code) -> UUID | None`; `live_link_for(session, org_id, *, code, source, now, for_update=False) -> InstallLink | None`; `resolve_live(admin_engine, app_engine, *, code, source, now) -> UUID | None`; `switch_off_due(session, org_id, now) -> bool`; `console_base(public_console_url: str | None, host: str | None) -> str`; `link_url(base: str, code: str) -> str`.
- Test helpers (`tests/install_link_fixtures.py`): `make_csr_pem() -> str`, `FACTS: dict`, `peer_client(api_env, peer: str) -> httpx.AsyncClient`, `switch_on(api_env, org_id, networks, **extra) -> str` (returns the code).

```python
"""Shared helpers for the install-link tests (not a test module)."""

from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

import httpx
from cryptography import x509
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.serialization import Encoding
from cryptography.x509.oid import NameOID

#: The compose network Traefik sits on -- the default ITS_TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS.
TRAEFIK = "172.19.240.2"

FACTS: dict[str, Any] = {
    "hostname": "lab-pc-07",
    "os_family": "windows",
    "os_version": "11 24H2",
    "architecture": "amd64",
}


def make_csr_pem() -> str:
    key = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
    csr = (
        x509.CertificateSigningRequestBuilder()
        .subject_name(x509.Name([x509.NameAttribute(NameOID.COMMON_NAME, "enrolling-agent")]))
        .sign(key, None)
    )
    return csr.public_bytes(Encoding.PEM).decode()


def peer_client(api_env: Any, peer: str) -> httpx.AsyncClient:
    """A client whose TCP peer address is `peer` (ASGI `scope["client"]`)."""
    return httpx.AsyncClient(
        transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=api_env.app, client=(peer, 40000)),
        base_url="https://itms.example.test",
    )


async def switch_on(
    api_env: Any, org_id: UUID, networks: list[str], **extra: Any
) -> str:
    """Switch the link on through the API as an org-admin; return the code."""
    admin = await api_env.create_user(org_id, "org-admin")
    headers = await api_env.headers(org_id, admin)
    body = {"enabled": True, "networks": networks, "hourly_limit": 50} | extra
    response = await api_env.client.put("/api/v1/install-link", json=body, headers=headers)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    url = response.json()["url"]
    assert url is not None
    return str(url).rsplit("/i/", 1)[1]
```

```python
"""The install link's settings API (spec 2026-09-28 §1.1)."""

import hashlib
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.models import AuditEvent, InstallLink
from tests.install_link_fixtures import switch_on


async def _admin(api_env, org_id):
    sub = await api_env.create_user(org_id, "org-admin")
    return await api_env.headers(org_id, sub)


async def test_the_settings_are_admin_tier(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    tech = await api_env.headers(org_id, await api_env.create_user(org_id, "technician"))
    assert (await api_env.client.get("/api/v1/install-link", headers=tech)).status_code == 403
    put = await api_env.client.put(
        "/api/v1/install-link", json={"enabled": False}, headers=tech
    )
    assert put.status_code == 403


async def test_a_new_org_reads_a_switched_off_link_with_defaults(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    body = (await api_env.client.get("/api/v1/install-link", headers=await _admin(api_env, org_id))).json()
    assert body == {
        "enabled": False,
        "has_code": False,
        "networks": [],
        "disable_at": None,
        "hourly_limit": 50,
        "enabled_at": None,
        "joined_since_enabled": 0,
    }


async def test_it_cannot_be_switched_on_with_no_network(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    response = await api_env.client.put(
        "/api/v1/install-link",
        json={"enabled": True, "networks": []},
        headers=await _admin(api_env, org_id),
    )
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert "at least one network" in response.text


async def test_switching_on_mints_a_code_shown_once_and_stored_as_a_hash(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    assert len(code) == 32 and all(c.isalnum() or c in "-_" for c in code)

    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        row = (await session.execute(select(InstallLink).where(InstallLink.org_id == org_id))).scalar_one()
    assert row.code_hash == hashlib.sha256(code.encode()).hexdigest()
    assert code not in repr(row.__dict__)

    headers = await _admin(api_env, org_id)
    got = (await api_env.client.get("/api/v1/install-link", headers=headers)).json()
    assert got["enabled"] is True and got["has_code"] is True
    assert "url" not in got and code not in str(got)

    again = await api_env.client.put(
        "/api/v1/install-link",
        json={"enabled": True, "networks": ["10.50.0.0/16", "fd00::/8"], "hourly_limit": 20},
        headers=headers,
    )
    assert again.status_code == 200 and again.json()["url"] is None


async def test_the_link_url_uses_the_configured_console(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    api_env.settings.public_console_url = "https://itms.example.com/"
    headers = await _admin(api_env, org_id)
    response = await api_env.client.put(
        "/api/v1/install-link", json={"enabled": True, "networks": ["10.0.0.0/8"]}, headers=headers
    )
    assert response.json()["url"].startswith("https://itms.example.com/i/")


async def test_regenerate_replaces_the_code(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    old = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    response = await api_env.client.post(
        "/api/v1/install-link/regenerate", headers=await _admin(api_env, org_id)
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200
    new = response.json()["url"].rsplit("/i/", 1)[1]
    assert new != old
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        stored = (await session.execute(select(InstallLink.code_hash).where(InstallLink.org_id == org_id))).scalar_one()
    assert stored == hashlib.sha256(new.encode()).hexdigest()


async def test_bad_settings_are_refused_with_a_sentence(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    headers = await _admin(api_env, org_id)
    past = (datetime.now(UTC) - timedelta(minutes=1)).isoformat()
    for body, fragment in [
        ({"enabled": True, "networks": ["10.0.0.1/24"]}, "not a network"),
        ({"enabled": True, "networks": ["10.0.0.0/24"], "disable_at": past}, "future"),
        ({"enabled": True, "networks": ["10.0.0.0/24"], "hourly_limit": 0}, "hourly_limit"),
        ({"enabled": True, "networks": ["10.0.0.0/24"], "hourly_limit": 501}, "hourly_limit"),
    ]:
        response = await api_env.client.put("/api/v1/install-link", json=body, headers=headers)
        assert response.status_code == 422, body
        assert fragment in response.text, response.text


async def test_every_change_is_audited_and_no_audit_row_carries_the_code(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    headers = await _admin(api_env, org_id)
    await api_env.client.put(
        "/api/v1/install-link",
        json={"enabled": True, "networks": ["10.60.0.0/16"], "hourly_limit": 10},
        headers=headers,
    )
    regenerated = await api_env.client.post("/api/v1/install-link/regenerate", headers=headers)
    new_code = regenerated.json()["url"].rsplit("/i/", 1)[1]
    await api_env.client.put(
        "/api/v1/install-link",
        json={"enabled": False, "networks": ["10.60.0.0/16"], "hourly_limit": 10},
        headers=headers,
    )
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        rows = (
            await session.execute(
                select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.org_id == org_id).order_by(AuditEvent.id)
            )
        ).scalars().all()
    actions = [r.action for r in rows if r.action.startswith("enrollment.link")]
    assert actions == [
        "enrollment.link_created",
        "enrollment.link_settings_changed",
        "enrollment.link_enabled",
        "enrollment.link_settings_changed",
        "enrollment.link_regenerated",
        "enrollment.link_disabled",
    ]
    disabled = [r for r in rows if r.action == "enrollment.link_disabled"][0]
    assert disabled.meta["reason"] == "operator"
    for row in rows:
        assert code not in str(row.meta) and new_code not in str(row.meta)


async def test_the_view_counts_machines_that_joined_since_it_was_switched_on(api_env) -> None:
    from app.models import Device

    org_id = await api_env.create_org("il")
    await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        session.add_all(
            [
                Device(org_id=org_id, hostname=f"pc{i}", link_joined_at=datetime.now(UTC),
                       link_joined_from="10.50.0.9", review_state="pending")
                for i in range(3)
            ]
        )
        await session.commit()
    body = (await api_env.client.get("/api/v1/install-link", headers=await _admin(api_env, org_id))).json()
    assert body["joined_since_enabled"] == 3
    assert body["enabled_at"] is not None
```

Note the expected audit order in `test_every_change_is_audited...`: `save()` writes `link_created` (code minted), then `link_settings_changed` (networks changed from `[]`), then `link_enabled`; the second PUT changes networks and limit only; the last PUT repeats the same networks and limit, so it writes `link_disabled` and nothing else.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the service**

`backend/app/services/install_link.py`:

```python
"""The install link: one shared, switchable enrollment link per organisation.

docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-install-link-and-windows-push-design.md §1.

WHAT THE LINK IS. A 32-character code that, while the link is on and the
request comes from a listed network, lets a machine enrol into THIS
organisation -- as a device waiting for review (§1.5). It is the only
authorization for its own enrollment, exactly as an enrollment token is (§0,
invariant 6), and it is handled like one: generated here, hashed, stored only as
the hash, and shown once.

WHAT BOUNDS IT (§1.6): the network list, the hourly limit, the on/off switch,
the optional automatic switch-off, regeneration -- and the review gate, which is
not here but in `app/jobs/review_gate.py`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import ipaddress
import secrets as pysecrets
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession

from app.core.audit import audit_service
from app.core.client_address import IPAddress, address_in
from app.core.tenancy import org_session
from app.models import Device, InstallLink

#: `secrets.token_urlsafe(24)` is 32 characters of [A-Za-z0-9_-]: 192 bits.
CODE_BYTES = 24
CODE_LENGTH = 32
DEFAULT_HOURLY_LIMIT = 50
MIN_HOURLY_LIMIT = 1
MAX_HOURLY_LIMIT = 500
MAX_NETWORKS = 64
RATE_WINDOW = timedelta(hours=1)
_CODE_ALPHABET = frozenset("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_")


class InstallLinkError(ValueError):
    """A setting the operator can correct. The route answers 422 with it."""


def new_code() -> str:
    code = pysecrets.token_urlsafe(CODE_BYTES)
    if len(code) != CODE_LENGTH:  # pragma: no cover - a property of token_urlsafe
        raise RuntimeError("token_urlsafe(24) did not produce 32 characters")
    return code


def hash_code(code: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(code.encode()).hexdigest()


def looks_like_code(value: str) -> bool:
    """The code's shape, checked before any hashing or database work."""
    return len(value) == CODE_LENGTH and all(c in _CODE_ALPHABET for c in value)


def normalise_networks(values: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    if len(values) > MAX_NETWORKS:
        raise InstallLinkError(f"at most {MAX_NETWORKS} networks may be listed")
    out: list[str] = []
    for raw in values:
        item = raw.strip()
        try:
            network = ipaddress.ip_network(item, strict=True)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise InstallLinkError(f"{item!r} is not a network ({exc})") from exc
        if str(network) not in out:
            out.append(str(network))
    return out


def is_live(link: InstallLink, now: datetime) -> bool:
    """On, with a code, and not past its automatic switch-off. The beat that
    flips `enabled` at `disable_at` only RECORDS the switch-off; the link stops
    answering at that instant whether or not the beat has run."""
    return bool(
        link.enabled
        and link.code_hash
        and (link.disable_at is None or link.disable_at > now)
    )


def console_base(public_console_url: str | None, host: str | None) -> str:
    configured = (public_console_url or "").strip().rstrip("/")
    if configured:
        return configured
    return f"https://{(host or 'localhost').strip()}"


def link_url(base: str, code: str) -> str:
    return f"{base.rstrip('/')}/i/{code}"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LinkView:
    enabled: bool
    has_code: bool
    networks: list[str]
    disable_at: datetime | None
    hourly_limit: int
    enabled_at: datetime | None
    joined_since_enabled: int


async def _row(
    session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, *, for_update: bool = False
) -> InstallLink | None:
    query = select(InstallLink).where(InstallLink.org_id == org_id)
    if for_update:
        query = query.with_for_update()
    return (await session.execute(query)).scalar_one_or_none()


async def _joined_since(session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, since: datetime) -> int:
    count = (
        await session.execute(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(Device)
            .where(Device.org_id == org_id, Device.link_joined_at >= since)
        )
    ).scalar_one()
    return int(count)


async def view(session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, now: datetime) -> LinkView:
    row = await _row(session, org_id)
    if row is None:
        return LinkView(False, False, [], None, DEFAULT_HOURLY_LIMIT, None, 0)
    live = is_live(row, now)
    joined = (
        await _joined_since(session, org_id, row.enabled_at)
        if live and row.enabled_at is not None
        else 0
    )
    return LinkView(
        enabled=live,
        has_code=row.code_hash is not None,
        networks=[str(n) for n in row.networks],
        disable_at=row.disable_at,
        hourly_limit=row.hourly_limit,
        enabled_at=row.enabled_at if live else None,
        joined_since_enabled=joined,
    )


async def _audit(
    session: AsyncSession,
    org_id: UUID,
    actor_id: UUID | None,
    action: str,
    metadata: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    await audit_service.record(
        session,
        org_id=org_id,
        actor_type="user" if actor_id is not None else "system",
        actor_id=actor_id,
        action=action,
        target_type="install_link",
        target_id=str(org_id),
        metadata=metadata,
    )


async def save(
    session: AsyncSession,
    *,
    org_id: UUID,
    actor_id: UUID,
    enabled: bool,
    networks: list[str],
    disable_at: datetime | None,
    hourly_limit: int,
    now: datetime,
) -> str | None:
    """Write the settings. Returns the code if this call minted one, else None.

    Validation happens BEFORE the row is touched, so a refused save changes
    nothing and audits nothing.
    """
    nets = normalise_networks(networks)
    if not MIN_HOURLY_LIMIT <= hourly_limit <= MAX_HOURLY_LIMIT:
        raise InstallLinkError(
            f"hourly_limit must be between {MIN_HOURLY_LIMIT} and {MAX_HOURLY_LIMIT}"
        )
    if disable_at is not None and disable_at <= now:
        raise InstallLinkError("the automatic switch-off must be in the future")
    if enabled and not nets:
        raise InstallLinkError(
            "at least one network is required to switch the link on; an empty list "
            "would not mean 'anywhere'"
        )

    row = await _row(session, org_id, for_update=True)
    if row is None:
        row = InstallLink(org_id=org_id, created_by=actor_id, networks=[])
        session.add(row)
    was_on = is_live(row, now)
    before = {
        "networks": [str(n) for n in row.networks],
        "disable_at": row.disable_at.isoformat() if row.disable_at else None,
        "hourly_limit": row.hourly_limit if row.id else DEFAULT_HOURLY_LIMIT,
    }

    minted: str | None = None
    if enabled and row.code_hash is None:
        minted = new_code()
        row.code_hash = hash_code(minted)
    row.networks = nets
    row.disable_at = disable_at
    row.hourly_limit = hourly_limit
    row.enabled = enabled
    if enabled and not was_on:
        row.enabled_at = now
    if not enabled:
        row.enabled_at = None
    row.updated_at = now
    await session.flush()

    after = {
        "networks": nets,
        "disable_at": disable_at.isoformat() if disable_at else None,
        "hourly_limit": hourly_limit,
    }
    if minted is not None:
        await _audit(session, org_id, actor_id, "enrollment.link_created", {})
    changed = {k: {"before": before[k], "after": after[k]} for k in after if before[k] != after[k]}
    if changed:
        await _audit(session, org_id, actor_id, "enrollment.link_settings_changed", changed)
    if enabled and not was_on:
        await _audit(session, org_id, actor_id, "enrollment.link_enabled", {"networks": nets})
    if was_on and not enabled:
        await _audit(session, org_id, actor_id, "enrollment.link_disabled", {"reason": "operator"})
    return minted


async def regenerate(
    session: AsyncSession, *, org_id: UUID, actor_id: UUID, now: datetime
) -> str:
    """A new code; the old one stops working the moment this commits."""
    row = await _row(session, org_id, for_update=True)
    if row is None:
        row = InstallLink(org_id=org_id, created_by=actor_id, networks=[])
        session.add(row)
    code = new_code()
    row.code_hash = hash_code(code)
    row.updated_at = now
    await session.flush()
    await _audit(
        session, org_id, actor_id, "enrollment.link_regenerated", {"enabled": bool(row.enabled)}
    )
    return code


async def resolve_org(admin_engine: AsyncEngine, code: str) -> UUID | None:
    """Which organisation this code belongs to -- or None.

    ADMIN ENGINE, READ-ONLY, and it only picks the tenant: exactly the role
    `services/enrollment.py::enroll` gives the token id. Whether the link may
    be USED is decided inside that tenant's own session by `live_link_for`.
    """
    if not looks_like_code(code):
        return None
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        org_id: UUID | None = (
            await session.execute(
                select(InstallLink.org_id).where(InstallLink.code_hash == hash_code(code))
            )
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
    return org_id


async def live_link_for(
    session: AsyncSession,
    org_id: UUID,
    *,
    code: str,
    source: IPAddress | None,
    now: datetime,
    for_update: bool = False,
) -> InstallLink | None:
    """The link, if it may be used right now by a request from `source`."""
    row = await _row(session, org_id, for_update=for_update)
    if row is None or row.code_hash != hash_code(code) or not is_live(row, now):
        return None
    if not address_in(source, row.networks):
        return None
    return row


async def resolve_live(
    admin_engine: AsyncEngine,
    app_engine: AsyncEngine,
    *,
    code: str,
    source: IPAddress | None,
    now: datetime,
) -> UUID | None:
    org_id = await resolve_org(admin_engine, code)
    if org_id is None:
        return None
    async with org_session(org_id, engine=app_engine) as session:
        row = await live_link_for(session, org_id, code=code, source=source, now=now)
    return org_id if row is not None else None


async def switch_off_due(session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, now: datetime) -> bool:
    """The worker beat's half of the automatic switch-off: record it."""
    row = await _row(session, org_id, for_update=True)
    if row is None or not row.enabled or row.disable_at is None or row.disable_at > now:
        return False
    disable_at = row.disable_at
    row.enabled = False
    row.enabled_at = None
    row.updated_at = now
    await session.flush()
    await _audit(
        session,
        org_id,
        None,
        "enrollment.link_disabled",
        {"reason": "scheduled", "disable_at": disable_at.isoformat()},
    )
    return True
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the routes**

`backend/app/api/v1/install_link.py`:

```python
"""The install link's settings (spec 2026-09-28 §1.1).

GATED `enrollment.link.manage` ON EVERY VERB, INCLUDING THE READ: the settings
say which networks may enrol machines into this organisation, which is not a
fact a technician needs.
"""

from datetime import UTC, datetime

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from app.api.deps import AuthContext, require_permission
from app.core.tenancy import org_session
from app.services import install_link as svc

router = APIRouter(prefix="/install-link", tags=["install-link"])


class InstallLinkOut(BaseModel):
    """This organisation's install link, WITHOUT the link itself.

    The full link is shown once, by the response that created its code
    (`InstallLinkSaved.url`); after that only its hash exists.
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    enabled: bool = Field(description="On right now: switched on and not past its switch-off time")
    has_code: bool = Field(description="Whether a link has ever been generated")
    networks: list[str] = Field(description="The networks a request must come from")
    disable_at: datetime | None = Field(description="When the link switches itself off")
    hourly_limit: int = Field(description="Most machines that may join per rolling hour")
    enabled_at: datetime | None = Field(description="When it was switched on, while it is on")
    joined_since_enabled: int = Field(description="Machines that joined since it was switched on")


class InstallLinkSaved(InstallLinkOut):
    """The settings after a change, and the link if this change created one."""

    url: str | None = Field(
        description="The full link. Present only in the response that generated its code"
    )


class InstallLinkUpdate(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    enabled: bool
    networks: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=svc.MAX_NETWORKS)
    disable_at: datetime | None = None
    hourly_limit: int = Field(
        default=svc.DEFAULT_HOURLY_LIMIT, ge=svc.MIN_HOURLY_LIMIT, le=svc.MAX_HOURLY_LIMIT
    )


def _out(view: svc.LinkView) -> InstallLinkOut:
    return InstallLinkOut(**vars(view))


def _saved(view: svc.LinkView, url: str | None) -> InstallLinkSaved:
    return InstallLinkSaved(**vars(view), url=url)


def _base(request: Request) -> str:
    return svc.console_base(
        request.app.state.settings.public_console_url, request.headers.get("host")
    )


@router.get("", response_model=InstallLinkOut)
async def get_install_link(
    request: Request,
    ctx: AuthContext = Depends(require_permission("enrollment.link.manage")),
) -> InstallLinkOut:
    """The install link's settings: whether it is on, where it answers, its limit."""
    async with org_session(ctx.org_id, engine=request.app.state.app_engine) as session:
        return _out(await svc.view(session, ctx.org_id, datetime.now(UTC)))


@router.put("", response_model=InstallLinkSaved)
async def put_install_link(
    body: InstallLinkUpdate,
    request: Request,
    ctx: AuthContext = Depends(require_permission("enrollment.link.manage")),
) -> InstallLinkSaved:
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    async with org_session(ctx.org_id, engine=request.app.state.app_engine) as session:
        try:
            minted = await svc.save(
                session,
                org_id=ctx.org_id,
                actor_id=ctx.user_id,
                enabled=body.enabled,
                networks=body.networks,
                disable_at=body.disable_at,
                hourly_limit=body.hourly_limit,
                now=now,
            )
        except svc.InstallLinkError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, str(exc)) from None
        view = await svc.view(session, ctx.org_id, now)
    return _saved(view, svc.link_url(_base(request), minted) if minted else None)


@router.post("/regenerate", response_model=InstallLinkSaved)
async def regenerate_install_link(
    request: Request,
    ctx: AuthContext = Depends(require_permission("enrollment.link.manage")),
) -> InstallLinkSaved:
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    async with org_session(ctx.org_id, engine=request.app.state.app_engine) as session:
        code = await svc.regenerate(session, org_id=ctx.org_id, actor_id=ctx.user_id, now=now)
        view = await svc.view(session, ctx.org_id, now)
    return _saved(view, svc.link_url(_base(request), code))
```

Note on `InstallLinkError` vs Pydantic: `hourly_limit` 0/501 is refused by the model (422 naming `hourly_limit`); host bits and a past `disable_at` are refused by the service (422 with the sentence).

```python
"""The review gate (install-link spec §1.5): the single place every job is born
refuses, for a device waiting for review, every type outside the allow-list."""

import ipaddress
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession

from app.core.signing import LocalEd25519Signer

from app.jobs import registry

def _live_registry() -> set[str]:
    from app.modules.remote.jobtypes import register_job_type as register_remote_job_type
    from app.worker import register_worker_job_types

    register_worker_job_types()
    register_remote_job_type()

def test_the_allow_lists_are_exactly_the_spec() -> None:

    }

def test_an_approved_device_or_an_unknown_one_is_never_refused() -> None:

def test_an_unrecognised_state_is_held_back_like_rejected() -> None:

async def test_every_registered_type_outside_the_list_is_refused_before_signing(
    admin_engine: AsyncEngine, app_engine: AsyncEngine, engine: JobEngine
) -> None:
    """ENUMERATES THE LIVE REGISTRY, so a job type registered tomorrow is
    refused for a pending device without anybody remembering to add it here.
    `params={}` is deliberate: the gate runs BEFORE params validation, so a
    type whose params are required still raises the gate's error."""

        assert info.value.job_type == job_type
        assert str(device_id) in str(info.value) and job_type in str(info.value)
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        written = (await session.execute(select(Job).where(Job.target_id == device_id))).scalars().all()
    assert written == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the gate**

`backend/app/jobs/review_gate.py`:

```python
"""Which job types a device waiting for review may receive.

Install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.5. A machine that joined through the install
link is VISIBLE and HELD BACK: it reports inventory and health, keeps its
certificate alive, and can be told to uninstall itself -- and nothing else. No
software, script, patch, backup, remote session, sweep, push or policy reaches
it until a person approves it.

THE GATE IS IN `JobEngine.create`, the one place every job is born, so no module
can forget it -- and a job type registered tomorrow is refused by default,
because the lists below are ALLOW-lists. `tests/test_review_gate.py` enumerates
the live registry to prove it.
"""

from uuid import UUID

ALLOWED_WHILE_PENDING: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        # Q4: the machine is visible.
        "inventory.collect",
        # Q4: health is reported. Monitoring only -- remediation is a separate
        # job type (an approved action's) and is refused.
        "health.policy.apply",
        "health.check.now",
        # The certificate must stay alive until someone decides.
        "agent.cert.renew",
        "agent.cert.install",
        # Reject.
        "agent.uninstall",
    }
)

#: Once rejected, the only thing left to say to the machine is "uninstall".
ALLOWED_WHILE_REJECTED: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"agent.uninstall"})

WAITING_FOR_REVIEW = "waiting for review"


class DeviceAwaitingReview(Exception):
    """A job this device may not receive until a person approves it."""

    def __init__(self, device_id: UUID, job_type: str, review_state: str) -> None:
        self.device_id = device_id
        self.job_type = job_type
        self.review_state = review_state
        super().__init__(
            f"{WAITING_FOR_REVIEW}: device {device_id} is {review_state} and may not "
            f"receive {job_type}"
        )


def refused(review_state: str | None, job_type: str) -> bool:
    """`None` is "no such device in this org", which is not this gate's
    question: the engine has always created a job for whatever id it is given,
    and the callers that care check existence themselves."""
    if review_state is None or review_state == "approved":
        return False
    if review_state == "pending":
        return job_type not in ALLOWED_WHILE_PENDING
    return job_type not in ALLOWED_WHILE_REJECTED
```

```python
from app.models import Device, Job, JobStatus
```

```python
        # THE REVIEW GATE (install-link spec §1.5). Before params are validated
        # and before anything is written or signed: a refused job leaves no
        # row, no signature and no audit event behind it.
        review_state = (
            await session.execute(
                select(Device.review_state).where(
                    Device.id == target_device_id, Device.org_id == org_id
                )
            )
        ).scalar_one_or_none()

            raise DeviceAwaitingReview(target_device_id, type, review_state or "")
```

`backend/app/main.py`, directly after `app.middleware("http")(request_context_middleware)`:

```python
    # A job refused by the review gate is a 409, wherever it was raised: the
    # kernel route, a module route, the AI confirm path. One handler, so no
    # route has to remember to translate it.
    from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    from starlette.requests import Request as StarletteRequest

    from app.jobs.review_gate import WAITING_FOR_REVIEW, DeviceAwaitingReview

    async def _awaiting_review(_request: StarletteRequest, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
        assert isinstance(exc, DeviceAwaitingReview)
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=409,
            content={
                "detail": WAITING_FOR_REVIEW,
                "device_id": str(exc.device_id),
                "job_type": exc.job_type,
            },
        )

    app.add_exception_handler(DeviceAwaitingReview, _awaiting_review)
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`backend/tests/test_review_sweeps.py`:

```python
"""A device waiting for review never stalls an automatic dispatcher (plan D4).

Each test puts an approved and a pending device side by side where the sweep
would pick both, and asserts the approved one is still served and the pending
one is passed over -- rather than the whole org's cycle dying on the gate.
"""

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

import pytest
import redis.asyncio as aioredis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession

from app.core.signing import LocalEd25519Signer
from app.core.tenancy import org_session
from app.jobs.engine import JobEngine
from tests.ipam_fixtures import (
    seed_agent,
    seed_device,
    seed_org,
    seed_subnet,
    seed_vrf,
    upsert_observation_row,
)

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 28, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


class _RefusingEngine:
    """A job engine a test expects never to be asked for anything."""

    default_ttl_s = 900

    async def create(self, session: Any, **kw: Any) -> Any:
        raise AssertionError(f"no job should be created, got {kw['type']}")


async def _pair(admin_engine: AsyncEngine, tag: str, **device_kw: Any) -> tuple[UUID, UUID, UUID]:
    org = await seed_org(admin_engine, tag)
    approved = await seed_device(admin_engine, org.org_id, **device_kw)
    pending = await seed_device(admin_engine, org.org_id, review_state="pending", **device_kw)
    for device_id in (approved, pending):
        await seed_agent(admin_engine, org.org_id, device_id, status="online", last_seen_at=NOW)
    return org.org_id, approved, pending


async def test_the_patch_scan_sweep_scans_the_approved_and_passes_over_the_pending(
    admin_engine: AsyncEngine, app_engine: AsyncEngine, tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    from app import worker
    from app.modules.patch.handler import register_job_types

    register_job_types()
    org_id, approved, pending = await _pair(admin_engine, "rs-scan")
    published: list[UUID] = []

    async def fake_publish_job(_redis: Any, device_id: UUID, _envelope: Any) -> None:
        published.append(device_id)

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "publish_job", fake_publish_job)
    redis = aioredis.from_url("redis://localhost:6379/9", decode_responses=True)
    try:
        count = await worker.patch_scan_sweep_org(
            org_id,
            JobEngine(signer=LocalEd25519Signer(tmp_path / "k.key")),
            app_engine,
            redis,
            now=NOW,
        )
    finally:
        await redis.aclose()
    assert count == 1 and published == [approved]
    assert pending not in published
```

```python
async def test_a_remediation_is_skipped_for_a_device_waiting_for_review(
    admin_engine, tmp_path
) -> None:
    """Health is REPORTED for a pending machine (spec §1.5), so alerts open on
    it -- but remediation is not monitoring, and a machine-fired approved
    action must be passed over rather than stall the action sweep on the gate."""
    from sqlalchemy import update

    from app.models import Device, Job
    from app.modules.health.models import HealthAlertAction

    fixture, _ = await _armed_with_ticketing(admin_engine)
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    alert_id = await _open_one(admin_engine, fixture, now=now)
    action_id = await seed_approved_action(
        admin_engine, fixture.org_id, fixture.org.user_id, autonomy="autonomous",
        job_type="patch.scan",
    )
    row_id = await seed_action_row(
        admin_engine, fixture.org_id, alert_id, "remediate", approved_action_id=action_id
    )
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        await session.execute(
            update(Device).where(Device.id == fixture.device_id).values(review_state="pending")
        )
        await session.commit()

    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:
        await execute_pending_actions(session, fixture.org_id, job_engine=_engine(tmp_path), now=now)
        await session.commit()

    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        jobs = (await session.execute(select(Job).where(Job.org_id == fixture.org_id))).scalars().all()
        row = await session.get(HealthAlertAction, row_id)
    assert jobs == []
    assert row is not None and row.state == "skipped"
    assert "waiting for review" in (row.last_error or "")
```

**Files:**
