- Modify: `backend/tests/test_notification_delivery.py` (`_EVENT_TYPE_SAMPLE_PAYLOADS`)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_enrollment_link.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `link_svc.resolve_org`, `link_svc.live_link_for`, `link_svc.RATE_WINDOW` (Task 3); `client_address`, `app.state.trusted_proxies` (Task 1); `Device.review_state/link_joined_*` (Task 2).
- Produces: `app.services.enrollment.enroll_via_link(*, admin_engine, app_engine, ca, signer, gateway_url, link_code, source, csr_pem, facts, capabilities=None, now=None) -> EnrollResult`; exceptions `LinkNotFound`, `LinkRateLimited`. `EnrollRequest.token: str | None`, `EnrollRequest.link_code: str | None` — exactly one required. Event `device.joined_via_link` with payload keys `device_id, hostname, os_family, source_address, joined_at`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`backend/tests/test_enrollment_link.py`:

```python
"""POST /enroll/v1 with `link_code` (install-link spec §1.4, §5 Part 1)."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from uuid import UUID

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.models import Agent, AuditEvent, Device, DomainEvent, InstallLink
from tests.install_link_fixtures import FACTS, TRAEFIK, make_csr_pem, peer_client, switch_on

NOT_FOUND = {"detail": "Not Found"}
INSIDE = "203.0.113.5, 10.50.0.9"  # rightmost (Traefik's) is inside 10.50.0.0/16


def _body(code: str) -> dict:
    return {
        "link_code": code,
        "csr_pem": make_csr_pem(),
        "device_facts": FACTS,
        "capabilities": {"agent.uninstall": True, "inventory.collect": True},
    }


async def _enroll(api_env, code: str, *, peer: str = TRAEFIK, xff: str | None = INSIDE):
    async with peer_client(api_env, peer) as client:
        headers = {"X-Forwarded-For": xff} if xff is not None else {}
        return await client.post("/enroll/v1", json=_body(code), headers=headers)


async def test_a_link_enrollment_creates_a_pending_device_with_a_certificate(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("lnk")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    response = await _enroll(api_env, code)
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    body = response.json()
    device_id = UUID(body["device_id"])
    assert body["cert_pem"].startswith("-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE")

    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        device = await session.get(Device, device_id)
        agent = (await session.execute(select(Agent).where(Agent.device_id == device_id))).scalar_one()
        audit = (
            await session.execute(
                select(AuditEvent).where(
                    AuditEvent.org_id == org_id, AuditEvent.action == "agent.enrolled_via_link"
                )
            )
        ).scalar_one()
        event = (
            await session.execute(
                select(DomainEvent).where(
                    DomainEvent.org_id == org_id, DomainEvent.event_type == "device.joined_via_link"
                )
            )
        ).scalar_one()
    assert device is not None and device.org_id == org_id
    assert (device.review_state, device.provenance, device.source) == ("pending", "agent", "agent")
    assert str(device.link_joined_from) == "10.50.0.9" and device.link_joined_at is not None
    assert agent.capabilities == {"agent.uninstall": True, "inventory.collect": True}
    assert audit.meta["source_address"] == "10.50.0.9"
    assert code not in str(audit.meta)
    assert event.payload["device_id"] == str(device_id)
    assert event.payload["source_address"] == "10.50.0.9"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["off", "wrong_code", "outside", "switched_off_by_time", "malformed"])
async def test_every_refusal_is_the_same_404(api_env, case: str) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("lnk")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    xff = INSIDE
    if case == "off":
        admin = await api_env.headers(org_id, await api_env.create_user(org_id, "org-admin"))
        await api_env.client.put(
            "/api/v1/install-link",
            json={"enabled": False, "networks": ["10.50.0.0/16"]},
            headers=admin,
        )
    elif case == "wrong_code":
        code = code[:-1] + ("A" if code[-1] != "A" else "B")
    elif case == "outside":
        xff = "10.50.0.9, 192.0.2.44"
    elif case == "switched_off_by_time":
        async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
            await session.execute(
                update(InstallLink)
                .where(InstallLink.org_id == org_id)
                .values(disable_at=datetime.now(UTC) - timedelta(seconds=1))
            )
            await session.commit()
    elif case == "malformed":
        code = "not-a-code"
    response = await _enroll(api_env, code, xff=xff)
    assert response.status_code == 404, response.text
    assert response.json() == NOT_FOUND


async def test_a_client_supplied_header_cannot_claim_an_allowed_network(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("lnk")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    # Through Traefik: the client's own claim is NOT the rightmost entry.
    assert (await _enroll(api_env, code, xff="10.50.0.9, 203.0.113.5")).status_code == 404
    # Straight to the API from outside the trusted set: the header is ignored.
    assert (await _enroll(api_env, code, peer="198.51.100.7", xff="10.50.0.9")).status_code == 404
    # The same peer, when it IS inside the network, is let in on its own address.
    assert (await _enroll(api_env, code, peer="10.50.3.3", xff=None)).status_code == 201


async def test_regenerating_stops_the_old_code_at_once(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("lnk")
    old = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    admin = await api_env.headers(org_id, await api_env.create_user(org_id, "org-admin"))
    new = (await api_env.client.post("/api/v1/install-link/regenerate", headers=admin)).json()["url"].rsplit("/i/", 1)[1]
    assert (await _enroll(api_env, old)).status_code == 404
    assert (await _enroll(api_env, new)).status_code == 201


async def test_the_fifty_first_enrollment_in_an_hour_is_429_and_audited_once(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("lnk")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    joined = datetime.now(UTC) - timedelta(minutes=10)
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        session.add_all(
            [
                Device(org_id=org_id, hostname=f"pc-{i}", review_state="pending",
                       link_joined_from="10.50.0.9", link_joined_at=joined)
                for i in range(50)
            ]
        )
        await session.commit()
    first = await _enroll(api_env, code)
    second = await _enroll(api_env, code)
    assert first.status_code == second.status_code == 429
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        audited = (
            await session.execute(
                select(func.count()).select_from(AuditEvent).where(
                    AuditEvent.org_id == org_id, AuditEvent.action == "enrollment.link_rate_limited"
                )
            )
        ).scalar_one()
        devices = (
            await session.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(Device).where(Device.org_id == org_id))
        ).scalar_one()
    assert audited == 1
    assert devices == 50


async def test_a_code_only_ever_enrols_into_its_own_org(api_env) -> None:
    org_a = await api_env.create_org("lnk-a")
    org_b = await api_env.create_org("lnk-b")
    code_a = await switch_on(api_env, org_a, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    await switch_on(api_env, org_b, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    response = await _enroll(api_env, code_a)
    assert response.status_code == 201
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        device = await session.get(Device, UUID(response.json()["device_id"]))
    assert device is not None and device.org_id == org_a


async def test_a_body_must_carry_exactly_one_credential(api_env) -> None:
    both = {"token": "x.y", "link_code": "A" * 32, "csr_pem": make_csr_pem(), "device_facts": FACTS}
    neither = {"csr_pem": make_csr_pem(), "device_facts": FACTS}
    assert (await api_env.client.post("/enroll/v1", json=both)).status_code == 422
    assert (await api_env.client.post("/enroll/v1", json=neither)).status_code == 422


async def test_the_link_path_never_links_onto_an_existing_asset(api_env) -> None:
    """Plan D3: agent-reported MACs are not evidence enough to hand an
    operator-vouched row to a machine nobody has reviewed."""
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("lnk")
    mac = "00:11:22:33:44:55"
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:
        # The `nics` shape `app/services/matching.py::_device_macs` reads.
        asset = Device(org_id=org_id, hostname="lab-pc-07", source="manual", provenance="operator",
                       nics={"items": [{"identifier": "eth0", "mac": mac}]})
        session.add(asset)
        await session.commit()
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    body = _body(code) | {"device_facts": FACTS | {"macs": [mac]}}
    async with peer_client(api_env, TRAEFIK) as client:
        response = await client.post("/enroll/v1", json=body, headers={"X-Forwarded-For": INSIDE})
    assert response.status_code == 201
    assert UUID(response.json()["device_id"]) != asset.id

    # CONTROL, so the assertion above cannot pass on a fixture the matcher
    # never reads: the TOKEN path, with the same MAC, does link to the asset.
    admin = await api_env.headers(org_id, await api_env.create_user(org_id, "org-admin"))
    token = (
        await api_env.client.post(
            "/api/v1/enrollment-tokens",
            json={"name": "control", "expires_in_hours": 1, "max_uses": 1},
            headers=admin,
        )
    ).json()["token"]
    linked = await api_env.client.post(
        "/enroll/v1",
        json={"token": token, "csr_pem": make_csr_pem(), "device_facts": FACTS | {"macs": [mac]}},
    )
    assert linked.status_code == 201 and UUID(linked.json()["device_id"]) == asset.id
```

`backend/app/services/enrollment.py`: add imports


and, after `class EnrollmentError`:

```python
class LinkNotFound(Exception):
    """The install link is off, past its switch-off time, the code is wrong or
    malformed, or the request came from outside every listed network. Always a
    404 with nothing to tell the cases apart (spec §1.1)."""


class LinkRateLimited(Exception):
    """This organisation's hourly limit of link enrollments is used up: 429."""
```

and at the end of the file:

```python
async def enroll_via_link(
    *,
    admin_engine: AsyncEngine,
    app_engine: AsyncEngine,
    ca: CAService,
    signer: SigningService,
    gateway_url: str,
    link_code: str,
    source: IPAddress | None,
    csr_pem: str,
    facts: DeviceFacts,
    capabilities: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> EnrollResult:
    """Enrol a machine through the organisation's install link (spec §1.4).

    ONE TRANSACTION: the link row is locked `FOR UPDATE`, so two concurrent
    enrollments cannot both be the 50th, and the device, its agent, the audit
    row and the notification event commit together or not at all.

    THE DEVICE IS ALWAYS NEW (plan D3). `resolve_enrolment_target` is not
    consulted: it links on agent-reported MACs, and a machine nobody has
    reviewed must not inherit a row somebody vouched for.

    A 429 IS COMMITTED BEFORE IT IS RAISED, so the once-an-hour audit row is
    not rolled back with the refusal.
    """
    stamp = now or datetime.now(UTC)
    org_id = await link_svc.resolve_org(admin_engine, link_code)
    if org_id is None:
        raise LinkNotFound()

    limited = False
    created: tuple[UUID, Any] | None = None
    async with org_session(org_id, engine=app_engine) as session:
        link = await link_svc.live_link_for(
            session, org_id, code=link_code, source=source, now=stamp, for_update=True
        )
        if link is None:
            raise LinkNotFound()
        recent = (
            await session.execute(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(Device)
                .where(
                    Device.org_id == org_id,
                    Device.link_joined_at > stamp - link_svc.RATE_WINDOW,
                )
            )
        ).scalar_one()
        if recent >= link.hourly_limit:
            limited = True
            due = link.rate_limited_audit_at is None or (
                link.rate_limited_audit_at <= stamp - link_svc.RATE_WINDOW
            )
            if due:
                link.rate_limited_audit_at = stamp
                await audit_service.record(
                    session,
                    org_id=org_id,
                    actor_type="system",
                    actor_id=None,
                    action="enrollment.link_rate_limited",
                    target_type="install_link",
                    target_id=str(org_id),
                    metadata={"hourly_limit": link.hourly_limit, "source_address": str(source)},
                )
        else:
            device = Device(
                org_id=org_id,
                hostname=facts.hostname,
                os_family=facts.os_family,
                os_version=facts.os_version,
                architecture=facts.architecture,
                serial=facts.serial,
                source="agent",
                provenance="agent",
                review_state="pending",
                link_joined_from=str(source),
                link_joined_at=stamp,
                first_seen_at=stamp,
                last_seen_at=stamp,
            )
            session.add(device)
            await session.flush()
            issued = await ca.sign_csr(csr_pem, device_id=device.id, org_id=org_id)
            session.add(
                Agent(
                    org_id=org_id,
                    device_id=device.id,
                    cert_fingerprint=issued.fingerprint_sha256,
                    cert_serial=issued.serial,
                    cert_not_after=issued.not_after,
                    enrolled_at=stamp,
                    capabilities=capabilities or {},
                )
            )
            await audit_service.record(
                session,
                org_id=org_id,
                actor_type="agent",
                actor_id=None,
                action="agent.enrolled_via_link",
                target_type="device",
                target_id=str(device.id),
                metadata={
                    "hostname": facts.hostname,
                    "os_family": facts.os_family,
                    "source_address": str(source),
                    "cert_fingerprint": issued.fingerprint_sha256,
                },
            )
            await publish_event(
                session,
                org_id=org_id,
                event_type="device.joined_via_link",
                subject_type="device",
                subject_id=device.id,
                payload={
                    "device_id": str(device.id),
                    "hostname": facts.hostname,
                    "os_family": facts.os_family,
                    "source_address": str(source),
                    "joined_at": stamp.isoformat(),
                },
                actor_user_id=None,
            )
            created = (device.id, issued)

    if limited:
        raise LinkRateLimited()
    assert created is not None
    device_id, issued = created
    return EnrollResult(
        device_id=device_id,
        cert_pem=issued.cert_pem,
        chain_pem=issued.chain_pem,
        ca_bundle_pem=await ca.ca_bundle_pem(),
        gateway_url=gateway_url,
        job_signing_public_key_pem=await signer.public_key_pem(),
    )
```

(`func` and `select` are already imported in this file; confirm `from sqlalchemy import func, select, text, update`.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the route and the request model**

`backend/app/api/v1/enrollment.py`: add imports `from pydantic import model_validator` and `from app.core.client_address import client_address`. Change `EnrollRequest`:

```python
    #: The enrollment token. Every agent before 1.6.0 sends it, and still does.
    token: str | None = None
    #: The install link's code (spec 2026-09-28 §1.4) -- ADDITIVE: older agents
    #: never send it, and a body carrying it takes the link path instead of the
    #: token path. No pattern here on purpose: a malformed code is refused by
    #: the service with the same 404 as a wrong one, not with a 422 that says
    #: which.
    link_code: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=64)
    csr_pem: str
    device_facts: svc.DeviceFacts
    capabilities: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _exactly_one_credential(self) -> "EnrollRequest":
        if (self.token is None) == (self.link_code is None):
            raise ValueError("send exactly one of token or link_code")
        return self
```

Replace the body of `enroll_device` with:

```python
    state = request.app.state
    if body.link_code is not None:
        try:
            result = await svc.enroll_via_link(
                admin_engine=state.admin_engine,
                app_engine=state.app_engine,
                ca=state.ca,
                signer=state.signer,
                gateway_url=state.settings.gateway_url,
                link_code=body.link_code,
                source=client_address(request, state.trusted_proxies),
                csr_pem=body.csr_pem,
                facts=body.device_facts,
                capabilities=body.capabilities,
            )
        except svc.LinkNotFound:
            # FastAPI's own 404 body, so a refused link reads exactly like a
            # path that does not exist.
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, "Not Found") from None
        except svc.LinkRateLimited:
            raise HTTPException(
                status.HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
                "too many computers joined through this link in the last hour; try again later",
            ) from None
        return EnrollResponse(**result.__dict__)

    assert body.token is not None  # the validator guarantees exactly one
    try:
        result = await svc.enroll(
            admin_engine=state.admin_engine,
            app_engine=state.app_engine,
            ca=state.ca,
            signer=state.signer,
            gateway_url=state.settings.gateway_url,
            token=body.token,
            csr_pem=body.csr_pem,
            facts=body.device_facts,
            capabilities=body.capabilities,
        )
    except svc.EnrollmentError:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, "enrollment rejected") from None
    return EnrollResponse(**result.__dict__)
```

```python
def _render_device_joined_via_link(event: DomainEvent) -> tuple[str, str]:
    payload = event.payload
    name = payload.get("hostname") or "(unnamed computer)"
    return (
        f"[Toeshee] New computer waiting for review: {name}",
        "A computer joined through the install link and is waiting for review.\n\n"
        f"Computer: {name}\n"
        f"Operating system: {payload.get('os_family') or '(unknown)'}\n"
        f"Joined from: {payload.get('source_address') or '(unknown)'}\n"
        f"Joined: {payload.get('joined_at') or '(unknown)'}\n\n"
        "Until someone approves it, it receives only inventory, health monitoring and "
        "certificate renewal. Approve or reject it on the Devices page.\n",
    )
```

and in `_EMAIL_RENDERERS` add `"device.joined_via_link": _render_device_joined_via_link,`.

`backend/tests/test_install_link_page.py`:

```python
"""The public install-link page, its Windows download and its install.sh
(spec 2026-09-28 §1.2, §5 Part 1)."""

import base64
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.core.storage import ObjectStore
from app.modules.agent_distribution import link_page
from app.modules.agent_distribution.link_page import LinkRelease, pick_link_releases
from tests.install_link_fixtures import TRAEFIK, peer_client, switch_on

INSIDE = "203.0.113.5, 10.50.0.9"
SHA = "b" * 64
RELEASES = {
    ("windows", "amd64"): LinkRelease("1.6.2", "windows", "amd64", SHA),
    ("windows", "arm64"): LinkRelease("1.6.2", "windows", "arm64", SHA),
    ("linux", "amd64"): LinkRelease("1.6.2", "linux", "amd64", SHA),
    ("darwin", "arm64"): LinkRelease("1.6.2", "darwin", "arm64", SHA),
}


class _StubStore(ObjectStore):
    def __init__(self) -> None:  # noqa: D107 - never reaches for a bucket
        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def presign_agent_release_get(
        self, version: str, os_name: str, arch: str, *, expires_in: int,
        download_filename: str | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        self.calls.append(
            {"version": version, "os": os_name, "arch": arch,
             "expires_in": expires_in, "download_filename": download_filename}
        )
        return f"https://minio.test/agent-releases/{version}/its-agent-{os_name}-{arch}?sig=x"


@pytest.fixture()
def releases(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> dict:
    async def fake(_session: Any) -> dict:
        return dict(RELEASES)

    monkeypatch.setattr(link_page, "link_releases", fake)
    return RELEASES


async def _get(api_env, path: str, *, xff: str = INSIDE, headers: dict | None = None):
    async with peer_client(api_env, TRAEFIK) as client:
        return await client.get(path, headers={"X-Forwarded-For": xff} | (headers or {}),
                                follow_redirects=False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("suffix", ["", "/windows?arch=amd64", "/install.sh"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["off", "wrong_code", "outside"])
async def test_every_route_answers_the_same_404(api_env, releases, suffix: str, case: str) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("pg")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    xff = INSIDE
    if case == "off":
        admin = await api_env.headers(org_id, await api_env.create_user(org_id, "org-admin"))
        await api_env.client.put(
            "/api/v1/install-link", json={"enabled": False, "networks": ["10.50.0.0/16"]}, headers=admin
        )
    elif case == "wrong_code":
        code = code[:-1] + ("A" if code[-1] != "A" else "B")
    else:
        xff = "10.50.0.9, 192.0.2.44"
    response = await _get(api_env, f"/i/{code}{suffix}", xff=xff)
    unrouted = await _get(api_env, "/i-does-not-exist", xff=xff)
    assert response.status_code == unrouted.status_code == 404
    assert response.content == unrouted.content == b'{"detail":"Not Found"}'


async def test_the_page_offers_the_windows_download_and_the_command(api_env, releases) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("pg")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    response = await _get(api_env, f"/i/{code}")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.headers["content-type"].startswith("text/html")
    assert response.headers["cache-control"] == "no-store"
    assert response.headers["referrer-policy"] == "origin"
    assert "default-src 'none'" in response.headers["content-security-policy"]
    page = response.text
    assert f"/i/{code}/windows?arch=amd64" in page
    assert f"curl -fsSL https://itms.example.test/i/{code}/install.sh | sudo sh" in page
    assert "<script" not in page


async def test_an_arm_browser_is_offered_the_arm_download_first(api_env, releases) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("pg")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    page = (await _get(api_env, f"/i/{code}", headers={"Sec-CH-UA-Arch": '"arm"'})).text
    assert page.index("arch=arm64") < page.index("arch=amd64")


async def test_with_no_eligible_release_the_page_offers_nothing(api_env, monkeypatch) -> None:
    async def none(_session: Any) -> dict:
        return {}

    monkeypatch.setattr(link_page, "link_releases", none)
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("pg")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    page = (await _get(api_env, f"/i/{code}")).text
    assert "No Windows installer is available yet" in page
    assert "No macOS or Linux installer is available yet" in page
    assert "/windows?arch=" not in page and "install.sh" not in page


async def test_the_windows_download_is_a_15_minute_grant_named_for_the_code(api_env, releases) -> None:
    store = _StubStore()
    api_env.app.state.object_store = store
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("pg")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    response = await _get(api_env, f"/i/{code}/windows?arch=arm64")
    assert response.status_code == 302
    assert response.headers["location"].startswith("https://minio.test/agent-releases/1.6.2/")
    assert store.calls == [
        {"version": "1.6.2", "os": "windows", "arch": "arm64", "expires_in": 900,
         "download_filename": f"ITMS-Agent-Setup_{code}.exe"}
    ]


async def test_an_unknown_arch_is_the_same_404(api_env, releases) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("pg")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    response = await _get(api_env, f"/i/{code}/windows?arch=x86")
    assert response.status_code == 404 and response.json() == {"detail": "Not Found"}


async def test_install_sh_is_valid_sh_and_carries_the_code_only_as_data(api_env, releases) -> None:
    api_env.app.state.object_store = _StubStore()
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("pg")
    code = await switch_on(api_env, org_id, ["10.50.0.0/16"])
    response = await _get(api_env, f"/i/{code}/install.sh")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.headers["content-type"].startswith("text/plain")
    script = response.text
    assert f"CODE='{code}'" in script
    assert script.count(code) == 1
    assert "--token-stdin" in script and "--token " not in script
    assert "CONSOLE='https://itms.example.test'" in script
    assert f"sha='{SHA}'" in script
    assert "linux-arm64)" not in script  # no release published for it
    sh = shutil.which("sh")
    assert sh is not None
    checked = subprocess.run([sh, "-n"], input=script, text=True, capture_output=True)
    assert checked.returncode == 0, checked.stderr
    linux_b64 = script.split("printf %s '", 1)[1].split("'", 1)[0]
    shipped = Path(link_page.__file__).parent / "installers" / "linux" / "install.sh"
    assert base64.b64decode(linux_b64) == shipped.read_bytes()


class _Row:
    def __init__(self, version: str, os: str, arch: str, channel: str = "stable", sha: str = SHA):
        self.version, self.os, self.arch, self.channel, self.sha256 = version, os, arch, channel, sha


def test_only_a_stable_release_of_1_6_0_or_later_with_bytes_is_offered() -> None:
    picked = pick_link_releases(
        [
            _Row("1.5.9", "windows", "amd64"),
            _Row("1.6.0-rc1", "windows", "amd64"),
            _Row("1.7.0", "windows", "amd64", channel="pilot"),
            _Row("1.6.3", "windows", "amd64", sha=""),
            _Row("1.6.1", "windows", "amd64"),
            _Row("1.6.0", "windows", "amd64"),
            _Row("not-semver", "linux", "amd64"),
        ]
    )
    assert picked == {("windows", "amd64"): LinkRelease("1.6.1", "windows", "amd64", SHA)}
```

```python
"""The install link's public page, its Windows download and its install.sh.

Install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.2. PUBLIC BY DESIGN -- no sign-in -- which is
the operator's recorded decision overturning this module's spec §7 ("no
unauthenticated download endpoint"); `docs/modules/agent-distribution/spec.md`
§7 carries the amendment. What bounds it lives in
`app/services/install_link.py`: the link must be on, the code must match and the
request must come from a listed network, or every route here answers exactly
the 404 FastAPI gives a path that does not exist.

The machine fetches from this server; this module opens no connection to it.

WHAT THE PAGE SENDS BACK AND WHY:
  * `Referrer-Policy: origin` -- the download redirect goes to the object store,
    and a full referrer would put the code in its request log. It is also what
    the Windows self-installer reads back out of the file's Mark of the Web to
    learn which console it came from (plan D6).
  * `Accept-CH` / `Critical-CH: Sec-CH-UA-Arch` -- Chromium browsers retry the
    request once with the machine's architecture, so the page can offer the ARM
    build first on an ARM PC without a line of script.
  * No script at all, and a CSP that says so.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import html
import secrets
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Query, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, JSONResponse, PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse, Response
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.client_address import client_address
from app.core.tenancy import org_session
from app.models.core import AgentRelease
from app.modules.agent_distribution.api import _object_store
from app.modules.agent_distribution.service import DOWNLOAD_TTL_S, _installer_bytes
from app.modules.agent_update.semver import Version, parse_version, try_parse_version
from app.services import install_link as link_svc

router = APIRouter(include_in_schema=False)

#: The first agent that understands a link code (spec §4).
LINK_MIN_AGENT_VERSION: Version = parse_version("1.6.0")
WINDOWS_ARCHES = ("amd64", "arm64")
UNIX_PLATFORMS: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (
    ("linux", "amd64"),
    ("linux", "arm64"),
    ("darwin", "amd64"),
    ("darwin", "arm64"),
)
ARCH_LABEL = {"amd64": "64-bit Intel or AMD", "arm64": "ARM"}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LinkRelease:
    version: str
    os: str
    arch: str
    sha256: str


def pick_link_releases(rows: Iterable[Any]) -> dict[tuple[str, str], LinkRelease]:
    """Per (os, arch): the highest STABLE release, 1.6.0 or later, with bytes.

    `rows` are `AgentRelease` rows, or anything with the same five attributes
    (`version, os, arch, channel, sha256`) -- the unit test passes plain objects.
    """
    best: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[Version, LinkRelease]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        if row.channel != "stable" or not row.sha256:
            continue
        version = try_parse_version(row.version)
        if version is None or version < LINK_MIN_AGENT_VERSION:
            continue
        key = (row.os, row.arch)
        current = best.get(key)
        if current is None or version > current[0]:
            best[key] = (version, LinkRelease(row.version, row.os, row.arch, row.sha256))
    return {key: release for key, (_, release) in best.items()}


async def link_releases(session: AsyncSession) -> dict[tuple[str, str], LinkRelease]:
    rows = (
        await session.execute(
            select(AgentRelease).where(AgentRelease.channel == "stable", AgentRelease.sha256 != "")
        )
    ).scalars()
    return pick_link_releases(rows)


def windows_file_name(code: str) -> str:
    return f"ITMS-Agent-Setup_{code}.exe"


def _not_found() -> JSONResponse:
    # Byte-for-byte FastAPI's own 404 for an unrouted path.
    return JSONResponse({"detail": "Not Found"}, status_code=404)


async def _live_org(request: Request, code: str) -> UUID | None:
    state = request.app.state
    return await link_svc.resolve_live(
        state.admin_engine,
        state.app_engine,
        code=code,
        source=client_address(request, state.trusted_proxies),
        now=datetime.now(UTC),
    )


async def _releases_for(request: Request, org_id: UUID) -> dict[tuple[str, str], LinkRelease]:
    async with org_session(org_id, engine=request.app.state.app_engine) as session:
        return await link_releases(session)


def _console(request: Request) -> str:
    return link_svc.console_base(
        request.app.state.settings.public_console_url, request.headers.get("host")
    )


def _arch_hint(request: Request) -> str:
    hint = request.headers.get("sec-ch-ua-arch", "").strip().strip('"').lower()
    agent = request.headers.get("user-agent", "").lower()
    if hint == "arm" or "arm64" in agent or "aarch64" in agent:
        return "arm64"
    return "amd64"


def _on_windows(request: Request) -> bool:
    hint = request.headers.get("sec-ch-ua-platform", "").strip().strip('"').lower()
    return hint == "windows" or "windows" in request.headers.get("user-agent", "").lower()


_CSS = (
    "body{font:16px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;margin:0;background:#f6f7f9;color:#1c2330}"
    "main{max-width:40rem;margin:3rem auto;padding:0 1rem}"
    "section{background:#fff;border:1px solid #d9dee6;border-radius:10px;padding:1rem 1.25rem;margin:1rem 0}"
    ".btn{display:inline-block;background:#1f5eff;color:#fff;padding:.6rem 1rem;border-radius:8px;"
    "text-decoration:none;font-weight:600}"
    "pre{background:#10141c;color:#e8edf5;padding:.75rem;border-radius:8px;overflow-x:auto;"
    "white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all}"
)


def render_page(
    *,
    console: str,
    code: str,
    releases: Mapping[tuple[str, str], LinkRelease],
    windows_first: bool,
    arch: str,
    nonce: str,
) -> str:
    esc = html.escape
    windows = [a for a in WINDOWS_ARCHES if ("windows", a) in releases]
    if windows:
        primary = arch if arch in windows else windows[0]
        ordered = [primary] + [a for a in windows if a != primary]
        links = [
            f'<a class="btn" href="/i/{esc(code)}/windows?arch={primary}">'
            f"Download for Windows ({ARCH_LABEL[primary]})</a>"
        ] + [
            f'<p><a href="/i/{esc(code)}/windows?arch={a}">'
            f"Download for Windows ({ARCH_LABEL[a]}) instead</a></p>"
            for a in ordered[1:]
        ]
        win = (
            "".join(links)
            + "<p>Open the downloaded file and approve the prompt. Keep the file's name "
            "as it is: it is how the installer knows where to join.</p>"
        )
    else:
        win = (
            "<p>No Windows installer is available yet. Ask whoever runs ITMS to mark "
            "an agent release 1.6.0 or later as stable.</p>"
        )
    if any(key in releases for key in UNIX_PLATFORMS):
        command = f"curl -fsSL {console.rstrip('/')}/i/{code}/install.sh | sudo sh"
        unix = f"<p>Run this in a terminal on the computer:</p><pre>{esc(command)}</pre>"
    else:
        unix = (
            "<p>No macOS or Linux installer is available yet. Ask whoever runs ITMS to "
            "mark an agent release 1.6.0 or later as stable.</p>"
        )
    sections = [("Windows", win), ("macOS and Linux", unix)]
    if not windows_first:
        sections.reverse()
    body = "".join(f"<section><h2>{title}</h2>{content}</section>" for title, content in sections)
    return (
        '<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">'
        '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">'
        '<meta name="referrer" content="origin">'
        "<title>Install the ITMS agent</title>"
        f'<style nonce="{nonce}">{_CSS}</style></head><body><main>'
        "<h1>Install the ITMS agent</h1>"
        "<p>This installs the agent on the computer you are using now. It appears in "
        "ITMS within a minute and waits there until a technician approves it.</p>"
        f"{body}</main></body></html>"
    )


def _b64(rel: str) -> str:
    return base64.b64encode(_installer_bytes(rel)).decode("ascii")


def install_script(
    *,
    console: str,
    code: str,
    releases: Mapping[tuple[str, str], LinkRelease],
    presign: Callable[[LinkRelease], str],
) -> str:
    """A POSIX sh installer for this machine's platform.

    It downloads the published artefact, checks the SHA-256 this server
    embedded, and runs the SAME reviewed installer the install command runs
    (`_INSTALLERS_DIR`), handing it the code on stdin through the shell's own
    `printf` -- so the code is never an argument of any process.
    """
    cases = "\n".join(
        f"  {os_name}-{arch}) url='{presign(releases[(os_name, arch)])}'; "
        f"sha='{releases[(os_name, arch)].sha256}' ;;"
        for os_name, arch in UNIX_PLATFORMS
        if (os_name, arch) in releases
    )
    lines = [
        "#!/bin/sh",
        "# Installs the ITMS agent on this computer. Generated for one install link:",
        "# the code below is what lets this computer join, so do not pass this file on.",
        "set -eu",
        f"CONSOLE='{console.rstrip('/')}'",
        f"CODE='{code}'",
        "os=$(uname -s)",
        "arch=$(uname -m)",
        'case "$os/$arch" in',
        "  Linux/x86_64|Linux/amd64) key=linux-amd64 ;;",
        "  Linux/aarch64|Linux/arm64) key=linux-arm64 ;;",
        "  Darwin/x86_64) key=darwin-amd64 ;;",
        "  Darwin/arm64) key=darwin-arm64 ;;",
        '  *) echo "itms: $os $arch is not a platform the agent is published for" >&2; exit 1 ;;',
        "esac",
        "url=''",
        "sha=''",
        'case "$key" in',
        cases,
        "  *) : ;;",
        "esac",
        'if [ -z "$url" ]; then',
        '  echo "itms: no stable agent 1.6.0 or later is published for $key yet" >&2',
        "  exit 1",
        "fi",
        'if [ "$(id -u)" -ne 0 ]; then',
        '  echo "itms: run this with sudo" >&2',
        "  exit 1",
        "fi",
        "d=$(mktemp -d)",
        "trap 'rm -rf \"$d\"' EXIT",
        'curl -fsSL "$url" -o "$d/its-agent"',
        "if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then",
        "  got=$(sha256sum \"$d/its-agent\" | cut -d ' ' -f 1)",
        "else",
        "  got=$(shasum -a 256 \"$d/its-agent\" | cut -d ' ' -f 1)",
        "fi",
        'if [ "$got" != "$sha" ]; then',
        '  echo "itms: the download does not match its published checksum; nothing was installed" >&2',
        "  exit 1",
        "fi",
        'case "$key" in',
        "  linux-*)",
        f"    printf %s '{_b64('linux/install.sh')}' | base64 -d > \"$d/install.sh\"",
        f"    printf %s '{_b64('linux/its-agent.service')}' | base64 -d > \"$d/its-agent.service\"",
        "    ;;",
        "  darwin-*)",
        f"    printf %s '{_b64('darwin/install.sh')}' | base64 -D > \"$d/install.sh\"",
        f"    printf %s '{_b64('darwin/com.toeshee.itsagent.plist')}' | base64 -D > \"$d/com.toeshee.itsagent.plist\"",
        "    ;;",
        "esac",
        'chmod +x "$d/install.sh"',
        "# The code reaches the installer on stdin, through the shell's own printf.",
        "printf '%s' \"$CODE\" | bash \"$d/install.sh\" --token-stdin --url \"$CONSOLE\" --binary \"$d/its-agent\"",
        'echo "itms: installed. This computer appears in ITMS within a minute, waiting for review."',
        "",
    ]
    return "\n".join(lines)


_PAGE_HEADERS = {
    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
    "Referrer-Policy": "origin",
    "X-Frame-Options": "DENY",
    "Accept-CH": "Sec-CH-UA-Arch, Sec-CH-UA-Platform",
    "Critical-CH": "Sec-CH-UA-Arch",
    "Vary": "Sec-CH-UA-Arch, Sec-CH-UA-Platform, User-Agent",
}


@router.get("/i/{code}")
async def install_link_page(code: str, request: Request) -> Response:
    org_id = await _live_org(request, code)
    if org_id is None:
        return _not_found()
    nonce = secrets.token_urlsafe(16)
    page = render_page(
        console=_console(request),
        code=code,
        releases=await _releases_for(request, org_id),
        windows_first=_on_windows(request),
        arch=_arch_hint(request),
        nonce=nonce,
    )
    headers = _PAGE_HEADERS | {
        "Content-Security-Policy": (
            f"default-src 'none'; style-src 'nonce-{nonce}'; base-uri 'none'; "
            "form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'"
        )
    }
    return HTMLResponse(page, headers=headers)


@router.get("/i/{code}/windows")
async def install_link_windows(
    code: str, request: Request, arch: str = Query(default="amd64", max_length=8)
) -> Response:
    org_id = await _live_org(request, code)
    if org_id is None or arch not in WINDOWS_ARCHES:
        return _not_found()
    release = (await _releases_for(request, org_id)).get(("windows", arch))
    if release is None:
        return _not_found()
    url = _object_store(request).presign_agent_release_get(
        release.version,
        "windows",
        arch,
        expires_in=DOWNLOAD_TTL_S,
        download_filename=windows_file_name(code),
    )
    return RedirectResponse(
        url, status_code=302, headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store", "Referrer-Policy": "origin"}
    )


@router.get("/i/{code}/install.sh")
async def install_link_script(code: str, request: Request) -> Response:
    org_id = await _live_org(request, code)
    if org_id is None:
        return _not_found()
    releases = await _releases_for(request, org_id)
    store = _object_store(request) if any(k in releases for k in UNIX_PLATFORMS) else None

    def presign(release: LinkRelease) -> str:
        assert store is not None
        return store.presign_agent_release_get(
            release.version, release.os, release.arch, expires_in=DOWNLOAD_TTL_S
        )

    script = install_script(
        console=_console(request), code=code, releases=releases, presign=presign
    )
    return PlainTextResponse(script, headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store"})
```

Security note for the reviewer: `console_base` builds `https://<Host>` when `ITS_PUBLIC_CONSOLE_URL` is unset. Harden it in `app/services/install_link.py` so a Host header with anything but a hostname and port cannot reach the script's single-quoted `CONSOLE=` line:

```python
_HOST = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9.-]{1,253}(:[0-9]{1,5})?$")


def console_base(public_console_url: str | None, host: str | None) -> str:
    configured = (public_console_url or "").strip().rstrip("/")
    if configured:
        return configured
    candidate = (host or "").strip()
    return f"https://{candidate if _HOST.match(candidate) else 'localhost'}"
```

```python
def test_a_hostile_host_header_cannot_reach_the_script() -> None:
    from app.services.install_link import console_base

    assert console_base(None, "itms.example.com:443") == "https://itms.example.com:443"
    assert console_base(None, "evil'; rm -rf /; '") == "https://localhost"
    assert console_base("https://itms.example.com/", "ignored") == "https://itms.example.com"
```

- Produces: `device_list.DEVICE_STATES` gains `"review"`; `StateCounts.review: int`; `StateCountsOut.review: int = 0`; `DeviceOut.review_state: str = "approved"`, `DeviceOut.link_joined_from: str | None = None`, `DeviceOut.link_joined_at: datetime | None = None`. `GET /api/v1/devices?state=review` lists exactly the `pending` rows. - [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Append to `backend/tests/test_devices_list.py`:

```python
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Waiting for review (install-link spec §1.5, plan D1)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


async def test_the_review_tab_selects_the_pending_rows_and_a_rejected_row_is_nowhere(
    admin_engine: AsyncEngine,
) -> None:
    """Three rows that differ ONLY in `review_state`, so each assertion below
    is about that column and nothing else. *If the rejected tombstone leaked*,
    `all` would read 3 and the fleet tiles would count a machine a technician
    already threw out. *If the tab filtered on the wrong column*, `review`
    would select the approved row too."""
    org_id = await _org(admin_engine, "dl-review")
    await _device(admin_engine, org_id, hostname="ok-1", agent_status="online")
    await _device(
        admin_engine, org_id, hostname="new-1", agent_status="online",
        review_state="pending", link_joined_from="10.50.0.9", link_joined_at=NOW,
    )
    await _device(
        admin_engine, org_id, hostname="gone-1", agent_status="online",
        review_state="rejected", rejected_at=NOW,
    )
    async with AsyncSession(admin_engine) as session:
        everything = await device_page(session, org_id, now=NOW)
        review = await device_page(session, org_id, state="review", now=NOW)

    assert sorted(d.hostname or "" for d, *_ in everything.rows) == ["new-1", "ok-1"]
    assert (everything.state_counts.all, everything.state_counts.review) == (2, 1)
    assert (everything.fleet.total, everything.fleet.managed, everything.fleet.online) == (2, 2, 2)
    assert [d.hostname for d, *_ in review.rows] == ["new-1"]
    assert review.total == review.state_counts.of("review") == 1


async def test_the_route_says_how_and_from_where_a_pending_row_joined(api_env) -> None:
    org_id = await api_env.create_org("dl-review-route")
    joined = datetime.now(UTC) - timedelta(minutes=5)
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        session.add_all(
            [
                Device(org_id=org_id, hostname="new-1", review_state="pending",
                       link_joined_from="10.50.0.9", link_joined_at=joined),
                Device(org_id=org_id, hostname="old-1"),
                Device(org_id=org_id, hostname="gone-1", review_state="rejected",
                       rejected_at=joined),
            ]
        )
        await session.commit()
    headers = await api_env.headers(org_id, await api_env.create_user(org_id, "read-only"))

    body = (await api_env.client.get("/api/v1/devices?state=review", headers=headers)).json()
    assert body["state_counts"]["review"] == 1
    assert body["state_counts"]["all"] == body["fleet"]["total"] == 2
    [item] = body["items"]
    assert item["review_state"] == "pending"
    assert item["link_joined_from"] == "10.50.0.9"
    assert item["link_joined_at"] is not None

    plain = (await api_env.client.get("/api/v1/devices", headers=headers)).json()
    by_name = {i["hostname"]: i for i in plain["items"]}
    assert set(by_name) == {"new-1", "old-1"}
    assert by_name["old-1"]["review_state"] == "approved"
    assert by_name["old-1"]["link_joined_from"] is None
```

`backend/app/services/device_list.py`:

```python
#: The tab strip, as a vocabulary. `all` is not a filter; the other five are.
#:
#: `assets` is rows with NO agent — warehouse stock, network gear, anything a
#: sweep recorded — and it is deliberately NOT part of `offline`: offline is a
#: claim about something that should be answering, and a switch is not offline
#: (asset-inventory spec §5).
#:
#: `review` is a machine that joined through the install link and that nobody
#: has approved yet (install-link spec §1.5). It is ALSO in `online`/`offline`:
#: the tabs were never a partition (`certs` overlaps them too).
DEVICE_STATES = ("all", "online", "offline", "assets", "certs", "review")
```

`StateCounts` gains the field and `of()` the key:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StateCounts:
    """What each tab would show FROM THE CURRENT SEARCH, tab not applied."""

    all: int
    online: int
    offline: int
    assets: int
    certs: int
    review: int

    def of(self, state: str) -> int:
        return {
            "all": self.all,
            "online": self.online,
            "offline": self.offline,
            "assets": self.assets,
            "certs": self.certs,
            "review": self.review,
        }[state]
```

```python
    # device-delete path exists and ~35 foreign keys point at `devices.id`, so
```

```python
    #: `approved | pending` (install-link spec §1.5). `pending` is a computer
    #: that joined through the install link and that nobody has approved yet:
    #: until someone does, it receives inventory, health monitoring and
    #: certificate renewal and nothing else (`app/jobs/review_gate.py`).
    #: `rejected` is never served -- the list hides the tombstone (plan D1).

    #: rightmost `X-Forwarded-For` entry from Traefik). NULL for every other way
    #: a row comes to exist -- which is how the console tells "joined through
    #: the link" from "enrolled with a token".
    link_joined_from: str | None = None
    #: When it joined through the install link. NULL otherwise.
    link_joined_at: datetime | None = None
```

```python
"""`agent.uninstall` (install-link spec §1.5, §4): an ordinary signed job type,
classified in every kernel map, reachable only through Reject."""

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from app.core.approved_actions import non_approvable_reason
from app.jobs.direct_dispatch import DIRECTLY_DISPATCHABLE_JOB_TYPES, module_governed_reason
from app.jobs.dispatch_permissions import dispatch_permission_for
from app.jobs.registry import AgentUninstallParams, AgentUninstallResult, get_job_type
from app.jobs.review_gate import ALLOWED_WHILE_PENDING, ALLOWED_WHILE_REJECTED

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def test_it_is_registered_and_carries_no_parameters() -> None:
    job_type = get_job_type("agent.uninstall")
    assert job_type.params_model is AgentUninstallParams
    assert AgentUninstallParams().model_dump() == {}
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        AgentUninstallParams(path="C:\\Windows")  # type: ignore[call-arg]
    assert job_type.result_handler is None
    assert job_type.cancellable is False
    assert job_type.retry_relinker is None


def test_the_result_says_the_removal_was_scheduled() -> None:
    assert AgentUninstallResult(scheduled=True).model_dump() == {"scheduled": True}


def test_only_reject_dispatches_it() -> None:
    assert "agent.uninstall" not in DIRECTLY_DISPATCHABLE_JOB_TYPES
    reason = module_governed_reason("agent.uninstall")
    assert reason is not None
    assert "/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/reject" in reason
    assert reason.rstrip().endswith("(devices.review)")
    assert dispatch_permission_for("agent.uninstall") == "devices.review"


def test_no_machine_or_ai_actor_may_name_it() -> None:
    reason = non_approvable_reason("agent.uninstall")
    assert reason is not None and "NEVER" in reason


def test_the_review_gate_lets_it_through_in_both_held_states() -> None:
    assert "agent.uninstall" in ALLOWED_WHILE_PENDING
    assert ALLOWED_WHILE_REJECTED == {"agent.uninstall"}


def test_the_exported_contract_is_committed() -> None:
    params = json.loads(
        (REPO / "docs/kernel/wire-protocol/AgentUninstallParams.schema.json").read_text()
    )
    result = json.loads(
        (REPO / "docs/kernel/wire-protocol/AgentUninstallResult.schema.json").read_text()
    )
    assert params["properties"] == {} and params["additionalProperties"] is False
    assert result["required"] == ["scheduled"]
```

```python
"""Approve and Reject (install-link spec §1.5, §4; plan D1, D2)."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.models import Agent, AuditEvent, Device, Job
from tests.ipam_fixtures import seed_agent, seed_device, seed_org


async def _pending(api_env, *, capabilities: dict | None = None, agent: bool = True):
    org = await seed_org(api_env.admin_engine, "rv")
    device_id = await seed_device(
        api_env.admin_engine, org.org_id, hostname="DESKTOP-7Q2M4KD", review_state="pending",
        link_joined_from="10.50.0.9", link_joined_at=datetime.now(UTC),
    )
    if agent:
        await seed_agent(
            api_env.admin_engine, org.org_id, device_id,
            capabilities=capabilities if capabilities is not None else {"agent.uninstall": True},
        )
    return org.org_id, device_id


async def _as(api_env, org_id, role: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    return await api_env.headers(org_id, await api_env.create_user(org_id, role))


async def _actions(api_env, org_id) -> list[AuditEvent]:
    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        return list(
            (
                await session.execute(
                    select(AuditEvent)
                    .where(AuditEvent.org_id == org_id, AuditEvent.action.like("device.%"))
                    .order_by(AuditEvent.id)
                )
            ).scalars()
        )


async def test_review_is_technician_tier(api_env) -> None:
    org_id, device_id = await _pending(api_env)
    for verb in ("approve", "reject"):
        response = await api_env.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/{verb}", headers=await _as(api_env, org_id, "read-only")
        )
        assert response.status_code == 403, verb
    approved = await api_env.client.post(
        f"/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/approve", headers=await _as(api_env, org_id, "technician")
    )
    assert approved.status_code == 200, approved.text


async def test_approve_makes_it_an_ordinary_device_and_is_audited(api_env) -> None:
    org_id, device_id = await _pending(api_env)
    headers = await _as(api_env, org_id, "technician")
    body = (await api_env.client.post(f"/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/approve", headers=headers)).json()
    assert body["id"] == str(device_id) and body["review_state"] == "approved"
    # The history of how it joined survives the approval.
    assert body["link_joined_from"] == "10.50.0.9"
    [row] = await _actions(api_env, org_id)
    assert row.action == "device.review_approved" and row.target_id == str(device_id)

    again = await api_env.client.post(f"/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/approve", headers=headers)
    assert again.status_code == 409
    assert again.json()["detail"] == "this computer is not waiting for review"

    # And the gate now lets anything through: `hello` was refused a moment ago.
    admin = await _as(api_env, org_id, "org-admin")
    job = await api_env.client.post(
        "/api/v1/jobs", json={"type": "hello", "device_id": str(device_id), "params": {}}, headers=admin
    )
    assert job.status_code == 201, job.text


async def test_reject_dispatches_a_signed_uninstall_and_revokes_nothing_yet(api_env) -> None:
    org_id, device_id = await _pending(api_env)
    response = await api_env.client.post(
        f"/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/reject", headers=await _as(api_env, org_id, "technician")
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["uninstall"] == "dispatched" and body["job_id"] is not None
    assert "uninstall itself" in body["message"]

    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        device = await session.get(Device, device_id)
        agent = (await session.execute(select(Agent).where(Agent.device_id == device_id))).scalar_one()
        job = await session.get(Job, UUID(body["job_id"]))
    assert device is not None and device.review_state == "rejected" and device.rejected_at is not None
    assert device.status != "decommissioned"
    # D2: revoking now would cut the connection the uninstall travels over.
    assert agent.cert_revoked is False
    assert job is not None and job.type == "agent.uninstall" and job.params == {}
    assert job.signature and job.target_id == device_id
    # 24 hours to receive it: an agent that is off tonight still gets it tomorrow.
    assert (job.expires_at - job.issued_at).total_seconds() == 24 * 3600

    [row] = await _actions(api_env, org_id)
    assert row.action == "device.review_rejected"
    assert row.meta["uninstall"] == "dispatched" and row.meta["job_id"] == body["job_id"]

    # Gone from the list at once (Task 8), and nothing but the uninstall may
    # reach it any more.
    listing = await api_env.client.get("/api/v1/devices", headers=await _as(api_env, org_id, "read-only"))
    assert str(device_id) not in listing.text
    admin = await _as(api_env, org_id, "org-admin")
    job_refused = await api_env.client.post(
        "/api/v1/jobs",
        json={"type": "inventory.collect", "device_id": str(device_id), "params": {}},
        headers=admin,
    )
    assert job_refused.status_code == 409


async def test_an_agent_too_old_to_uninstall_is_revoked_now_and_the_tech_is_told(api_env) -> None:
    org_id, device_id = await _pending(api_env, capabilities={"inventory.collect": True})
    response = await api_env.client.post(
        f"/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/reject", headers=await _as(api_env, org_id, "technician")
    )
    body = response.json()
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert body["uninstall"] == "manual" and body["job_id"] is None
    assert "remove the agent from the computer by hand" in body["message"]

    async with AsyncSession(api_env.admin_engine) as session:
        device = await session.get(Device, device_id)
        agent = (await session.execute(select(Agent).where(Agent.device_id == device_id))).scalar_one()
        jobs = (await session.execute(select(Job).where(Job.target_id == device_id))).scalars().all()
    assert device is not None and device.status == "decommissioned"
    assert agent.cert_revoked is True and jobs == []
    assert api_env.app.state.ca.is_revoked(agent.cert_serial)
    assert [r.action for r in await _actions(api_env, org_id)] == [
        "device.review_rejected",
        "device.rejection_completed",
    ]


async def test_only_a_pending_device_can_be_rejected_and_other_orgs_are_404(api_env) -> None:
    org_id, device_id = await _pending(api_env)
    headers = await _as(api_env, org_id, "technician")
    other = await seed_device(api_env.admin_engine, org_id, hostname="approved-pc")
    assert (
        await api_env.client.post(f"/api/v1/devices/{other}/review/reject", headers=headers)
    ).status_code == 409
    assert (
        await api_env.client.post(f"/api/v1/devices/{uuid4()}/review/reject", headers=headers)
    ).status_code == 404

    stranger_org = (await seed_org(api_env.admin_engine, "rv-b")).org_id
    stranger = await _as(api_env, stranger_org, "technician")
    assert (
        await api_env.client.post(f"/api/v1/devices/{device_id}/review/reject", headers=stranger)
    ).status_code == 404
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Register the job type and classify it everywhere**

`backend/app/jobs/registry.py`, after `class JobCancelResult`:

```python
class AgentUninstallParams(BaseModel):
    """`agent.uninstall` -- server -> agent (install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.5).

    CARRIES NOTHING, AND THAT IS THE DESIGN. What to remove is the agent's own
    knowledge -- its service, its binary, its data directory -- so there is no
    field an operator or a compromised control plane could point at a path.
    `extra="forbid"`: a member added here is a second input to a command path
    that deletes files, and needs a review that knows it.
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class AgentUninstallResult(BaseModel):
    """agent -> server: the removal has been SCHEDULED, not done.

    A process cannot delete its own running service and then report, so the
    capability starts a detached helper that waits, stops the service and
    removes everything, and answers before it does (plan D7). `succeeded` on
    this job is what `device_review.complete_due_rejections` reads as "the
    agent confirmed".
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    scheduled: bool
```

and at the end of the file:

```python
# Reject (install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.5, §4). An ORDINARY SIGNED JOB, like
# the certificate legs above: invariant #1 has no "it is only an uninstall"
# exemption. Created only by `app/services/device_review.py::reject`, only for
# an agent that advertises the capability (1.6.0+), and admitted by the review
# gate for a `pending` or `rejected` device -- the one type a rejected machine
# may still receive.
register(JobType("agent.uninstall", AgentUninstallParams))
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the review service**

`backend/app/services/device_review.py`:

```python
"""Approve or reject a computer that joined through the install link.

docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-install-link-and-windows-push-design.md §1.5
and §4; plan decisions D1 (a tombstone, not a DELETE) and D2 (revoke at
completion, not at the click).

APPROVE is one state change and one audit row. From then on the device is an
ordinary device and the review gate never looks at it again.

REJECT is three acts spread over time, because the one that has to happen first
-- telling the agent to remove itself -- travels over the connection that
revocation would cut:

  1. NOW: `review_state = 'rejected'`, audited, and for an agent that advertises
     `agent.uninstall` (1.6.0+) a signed `agent.uninstall` job valid for 24
     hours, so an agent that is switched off tonight still receives it tomorrow.
  2. WHEN THE AGENT CONFIRMS (the job `succeeded`) OR 24 HOURS LATER, whichever
     is first: the certificate is revoked and the row becomes a
     `decommissioned` tombstone. `complete_due_rejections`, run by a beat.
  3. AN AGENT TOO OLD TO UNINSTALL ITSELF skips the job: its certificate is
     revoked and the row tombstoned at once, and the technician is told to
     remove the agent by hand (spec §4).

THE CRL WRITE AND THE CONNECTION DROP HAPPEN IN THE CALLER, after the tenant
transaction commits, exactly as `POST /agents/{device_id}/revoke` does them:
`agents.cert_revoked` is the gateway's admission gate and is committed first,
so a CRL write that has to be retried fails safe.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.audit import audit_service
from app.jobs.engine import JobEngine
from app.models import Agent, Device, Job, JobStatus

UNINSTALL_JOB_TYPE = "agent.uninstall"
#: How long a rejected machine has to confirm its uninstall before its
#: certificate is revoked anyway (spec §1.5: "or after 24 hours").
UNINSTALL_GRACE = timedelta(hours=24)
#: The completion beat's cadence (Task 16).
COMPLETION_INTERVAL_S = 60.0

DISPATCHED_MESSAGE = (
    "The agent has been told to uninstall itself. Its certificate is revoked as soon "
    "as it confirms, or in 24 hours if it does not."
)
MANUAL_REMOVAL_MESSAGE = (
    "This computer's agent is too old to remove itself. Its certificate has been "
    "revoked, so it can no longer connect; remove the agent from the computer by hand."
)


class ReviewNotFound(LookupError):
    """No device with this id in this organisation. The route answers 404."""


class NotWaitingForReview(ValueError):
    """The device is not `pending`. The route answers 409."""


def can_uninstall(capabilities: object) -> bool:
    """Did the agent report `agent.uninstall`? Typed `object` for the reason
    `remote.capability.can_share_screen` gives: the column is JSON."""
    return isinstance(capabilities, dict) and bool(capabilities.get(UNINSTALL_JOB_TYPE))


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RejectOutcome:
    device_id: UUID
    #: `dispatched` (the agent will remove itself) or `manual` (it cannot).
    uninstall: str
    job: Job | None
    #: Publish AFTER the transaction commits.
    envelope: dict[str, Any] | None
    #: A certificate serial the caller must write to the CRL, when revoked now.
    revoke_serial: str | None
    message: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CompletedRejection:
    device_id: UUID
    #: `agent_confirmed` or `timeout`.
    reason: str
    revoke_serial: str | None


async def _pending(session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, device_id: UUID) -> Device:
    device = (
        await session.execute(
            select(Device)
            .where(Device.org_id == org_id, Device.id == device_id)
            .with_for_update()
        )
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    if device is None:
        raise ReviewNotFound(f"no device {device_id} in this organisation")
    if device.review_state != "pending":
        raise NotWaitingForReview("this computer is not waiting for review")
    return device


async def _agent(session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, device_id: UUID) -> Agent | None:
    agent: Agent | None = (
        await session.execute(
            select(Agent).where(Agent.org_id == org_id, Agent.device_id == device_id)
        )
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    return agent


async def approve(
    session: AsyncSession, *, org_id: UUID, actor_id: UUID, device_id: UUID, now: datetime
) -> Device:
    device = await _pending(session, org_id, device_id)
    device.review_state = "approved"
    session.add(device)
    await audit_service.record(
        session,
        org_id=org_id,
        actor_type="user",
        actor_id=actor_id,
        action="device.review_approved",
        target_type="device",
        target_id=str(device_id),
        metadata={
            "hostname": device.hostname,
            "link_joined_from": str(device.link_joined_from) if device.link_joined_from else None,
            "approved_at": now.isoformat(),
        },
    )
    await session.flush()
    return device


async def _tombstone(
    session: AsyncSession,
    *,
    org_id: UUID,
    device: Device,
    agent: Agent | None,
    reason: str,
    actor_id: UUID | None,
) -> str | None:
    """Revoke the certificate (the DB flag) and decommission the row."""
    serial: str | None = None
    if agent is not None:
        agent.cert_revoked = True
        session.add(agent)
        serial = agent.cert_serial
    device.status = "decommissioned"
    session.add(device)
    await audit_service.record(
        session,
        org_id=org_id,
        actor_type="user" if actor_id is not None else "system",
        actor_id=actor_id,
        action="device.rejection_completed",
        target_type="device",
        target_id=str(device.id),
        metadata={
            "reason": reason,
            "cert_serial": agent.cert_serial if agent is not None else None,
            "cert_fingerprint": agent.cert_fingerprint if agent is not None else None,
        },
    )
    await session.flush()
    return serial


async def reject(
    session: AsyncSession,
    job_engine: JobEngine,
    *,
    org_id: UUID,
    actor_id: UUID,
    device_id: UUID,
    now: datetime,
) -> RejectOutcome:
    device = await _pending(session, org_id, device_id)
    agent = await _agent(session, org_id, device_id)
    device.review_state = "rejected"
    device.rejected_at = now
    session.add(device)
    # FLUSHED BEFORE THE JOB IS CREATED: the review gate reads this row, and
    # `rejected` is the state that admits `agent.uninstall` and nothing else.
    await session.flush()

    capable = agent is not None and not agent.cert_revoked and can_uninstall(agent.capabilities)
    job: Job | None = None
    envelope: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    if capable:
        job = await job_engine.create(
            session,
            org_id=org_id,
            type=UNINSTALL_JOB_TYPE,
            target_device_id=device_id,
            params={},
            requested_by=actor_id,
            ttl_s=int(UNINSTALL_GRACE.total_seconds()),
        )
        envelope = await job_engine.signed_envelope(job)
    await audit_service.record(
        session,
        org_id=org_id,
        actor_type="user",
        actor_id=actor_id,
        action="device.review_rejected",
        target_type="device",
        target_id=str(device_id),
        metadata={
            "hostname": device.hostname,
            "link_joined_from": str(device.link_joined_from) if device.link_joined_from else None,
            "uninstall": "dispatched" if capable else "manual",
            "job_id": str(job.id) if job is not None else None,
        },
    )
    await session.flush()
    if capable:
        return RejectOutcome(device_id, "dispatched", job, envelope, None, DISPATCHED_MESSAGE)
    serial = await _tombstone(
        session,
        org_id=org_id,
        device=device,
        agent=agent,
        reason="agent_cannot_uninstall",
        actor_id=actor_id,
    )
    return RejectOutcome(device_id, "manual", None, None, serial, MANUAL_REMOVAL_MESSAGE)


async def complete_due_rejections(
    session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, *, now: datetime
) -> list[CompletedRejection]:
    """Tombstone every rejected device whose agent confirmed, or whose 24 hours
    are up. `SKIP LOCKED` so two beat workers never complete one row twice."""
    rows = (
        await session.execute(
            select(Device)
            .where(
                Device.org_id == org_id,
                Device.review_state == "rejected",
                Device.status != "decommissioned",
            )
            .with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
        )
    ).scalars().all()
    done: list[CompletedRejection] = []
    for device in rows:
        confirmed = (
            await session.execute(
                select(Job.id)
                .where(
                    Job.org_id == org_id,
                    Job.target_id == device.id,
                    Job.type == UNINSTALL_JOB_TYPE,
                    Job.status == JobStatus.succeeded,
                )
                .limit(1)
            )
        ).scalar_one_or_none() is not None
        timed_out = device.rejected_at is None or device.rejected_at <= now - UNINSTALL_GRACE
        if not (confirmed or timed_out):
            continue
        reason = "agent_confirmed" if confirmed else "timeout"
        serial = await _tombstone(
            session,
            org_id=org_id,
            device=device,
            agent=await _agent(session, org_id, device.id),
            reason=reason,
            actor_id=None,
        )
        done.append(CompletedRejection(device.id, reason, serial))
    return done
```

```python
async def _one_device_out(session: AsyncSession, org_id: UUID, device_id: UUID) -> DeviceOut:
    """`get_device`'s composition for one row, with the org predicate written out."""
    device, agent_status, cert_not_after, cert_revoked, agent_id, capabilities = (
        await session.execute(
            select(
                Device, Agent.status, Agent.cert_not_after, Agent.cert_revoked,
                Agent.id, Agent.capabilities,
            )
            .join(Agent, isouter=True)
            .where(Device.org_id == org_id, Device.id == device_id)
        )
    ).one()
    return _row_to_out(
        device, agent_status, cert_not_after, cert_revoked,
        screen_capable=_screen_capable(agent_id, capabilities),
    )


class DeviceRejectedOut(BaseModel):
    """What a Reject did (install-link spec §1.5, §4)."""

    device_id: UUID
    #: `dispatched`: the agent was told to uninstall itself; its certificate is
    #: revoked when it confirms or in 24 hours. `manual`: the agent is too old
    #: to remove itself; its certificate was revoked now and a technician has
    #: to remove it from the computer by hand.
    uninstall: Literal["dispatched", "manual"]
    job_id: UUID | None
    #: One sentence for the technician, saying which of the two happened.
    message: str


@router.post("/{device_id}/review/approve", response_model=DeviceOut)
async def approve_device(
    device_id: UUID,
    request: Request,
    ctx: AuthContext = Depends(require_permission("devices.review")),
) -> DeviceOut:
    """Approve a computer that joined through the install link. From now on it
    is an ordinary device and receives every job its organisation sends."""
    async with org_session(ctx.org_id, engine=request.app.state.app_engine) as session:
        try:
            await review_svc.approve(
                session, org_id=ctx.org_id, actor_id=ctx.user_id, device_id=device_id,
                now=datetime.now(UTC),
            )
        except review_svc.ReviewNotFound:
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, "no such device") from None
        except review_svc.NotWaitingForReview as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, str(exc)) from None
        return await _one_device_out(session, ctx.org_id, device_id)


@router.post("/{device_id}/review/reject", response_model=DeviceRejectedOut)
async def reject_device(
    device_id: UUID,
    request: Request,
    ctx: AuthContext = Depends(require_permission("devices.review")),
) -> DeviceRejectedOut:
    """Reject a computer that joined through the install link: its agent is told
    to uninstall itself and its certificate is revoked (spec §1.5)."""
    state = request.app.state
    async with org_session(ctx.org_id, engine=state.app_engine) as session:
        try:
            outcome = await review_svc.reject(
                session, state.job_engine, org_id=ctx.org_id, actor_id=ctx.user_id,
                device_id=device_id, now=datetime.now(UTC),
            )
        except review_svc.ReviewNotFound:
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, "no such device") from None
        except review_svc.NotWaitingForReview as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, str(exc)) from None
    # COMMITTED. Only now is anything published: a job handed to an agent before
    # its row is durable could complete against nothing.
    if outcome.envelope is not None:
        await publish_job(state.redis, device_id, outcome.envelope)
    if outcome.revoke_serial is not None:
        await state.ca.revoke(outcome.revoke_serial)
        await publish_revoke(state.redis, device_id)
    return DeviceRejectedOut(
        device_id=device_id,
        uninstall="dispatched" if outcome.uninstall == "dispatched" else "manual",
        job_id=outcome.job.id if outcome.job is not None else None,
        message=outcome.message,
    )
```

(`AsyncSession` comes from `sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio`; add it to the imports if the file does not already import it.)

`docs/kernel/wire-protocol/README.md`, a new section at the end:

```markdown
## Reject: `agent.uninstall` (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-install-link-and-windows-push-design.md` §1.5, §4)

A new job TYPE, not a new wire `kind` -- the `job.cancel` decision again. An
agent older than 1.6.0 refuses it `unknown_type`, loudly and before anything
runs; the control plane never sends it one in the first place, because
`app/services/device_review.py` creates the job only for an agent whose
`agents.capabilities` carries `agent.uninstall`, and revokes an older agent's
certificate instead.

**Params carry nothing** (`AgentUninstallParams`, `extra="forbid"`): what to
remove is the agent's own knowledge -- its service, its binary, its data
directory -- so no field exists that could point it at a path.

**`succeeded` means scheduled, not done** (`AgentUninstallResult.scheduled`).
The capability starts a detached helper that waits 15 seconds (so this result
reaches the gateway), then stops and deletes the service and removes the
binary and the data directory. The control plane reads the `succeeded` job as
the agent's confirmation and revokes the certificate within a minute; a result
lost with the data directory is covered by the 24-hour completion.
```
