```go
package uninstall_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"runtime"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/canonical"
	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/capabilities"
	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall"
	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/wire"
)

const testDeviceID = "6f1a2c34-5b6d-4e7f-8a9b-0c1d2e3f4a5b"

func quiet() *slog.Logger { return slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)) }

type launchCall struct{ binary, dataDir string }

type fakeLauncher struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	calls []launchCall
	err   error
}

func (f *fakeLauncher) launch(_ context.Context, binary, dataDir string) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.calls = append(f.calls, launchCall{binary, dataDir})
	return f.err
}

func (f *fakeLauncher) seen() []launchCall {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]launchCall(nil), f.calls...)
}

func bound(f *fakeLauncher) *uninstall.Service {
	return uninstall.New(uninstall.Options{
		Launch:  f.launch,
		Binary:  func() (string, error) { return "/usr/local/bin/its-agent", nil },
		DataDir: "/var/lib/its-agent",
		Logger:  quiet(),
	})
}

func TestTheJobTypeIsTheOneTheServerRegisters(t *testing.T) {
	if uninstall.JobType != "agent.uninstall" {
		t.Fatalf("JobType = %q; the server registers \"agent.uninstall\"", uninstall.JobType)
	}
}

func TestThisBuildAdvertisesIt(t *testing.T) {
	// The control plane sends `agent.uninstall` ONLY to an agent that
	// advertised it (device_review.can_uninstall); an older one is revoked
	// and removed by hand. Advertising it is therefore what makes Reject
	// clean on this OS.
	if !uninstall.Supported {
		t.Skipf("agent.uninstall is not supported on %s", runtime.GOOS)
	}
	if h, ok := capabilities.Lookup(uninstall.JobType); !ok || h == nil {
		t.Fatal("agent.uninstall is not in this build's registry")
	}
}

func TestASignedJobSchedulesTheHelperAndAnswersScheduled(t *testing.T) {
	f := &fakeLauncher{}
	svc := bound(f)
	h := newRunnerHarness(t, map[string]jobs.Handler{uninstall.JobType: svc.Run})
	h.deliver(t, uninstall.JobType, `{}`, "n-ok")
	res := h.awaitResult(t)
	if res["state"] != "succeeded" {
		t.Fatalf("state = %v, want succeeded; frames: %v", res["state"], res)
	}
	body, _ := res["result"].(map[string]any)
	if body["scheduled"] != true {
		t.Fatalf("result = %v, want {scheduled: true}", body)
	}
	if got := f.seen(); len(got) != 1 || got[0] != (launchCall{"/usr/local/bin/its-agent", "/var/lib/its-agent"}) {
		t.Fatalf("launcher calls = %v; the helper is told THIS agent's binary and data directory, "+
			"and nothing from the job", got)
	}
	if svc.Runs() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Runs() = %d, want 1", svc.Runs())
	}
}

func TestUnknownParamsAreIgnoredButANonObjectIsRefused(t *testing.T) {
	// The inbound half of the wire-protocol rule: a member a newer server adds
	// is ignored, so a strictly additive change does not break this fleet. A
	// params value that is not an object is not an additive change.
	f := &fakeLauncher{}
	h := newRunnerHarness(t, map[string]jobs.Handler{uninstall.JobType: bound(f).Run})
	h.deliver(t, uninstall.JobType, `{"future":1}`, "n-extra")
	if res := h.awaitResult(t); res["state"] != "succeeded" {
		t.Fatalf("an unknown member was not ignored: %v", res)
	}

	g := &fakeLauncher{}
	h2 := newRunnerHarness(t, map[string]jobs.Handler{uninstall.JobType: bound(g).Run})
	h2.deliver(t, uninstall.JobType, `[]`, "n-array")
	if res := h2.awaitResult(t); res["state"] != "refused" {
		t.Fatalf("state = %v, want refused", res["state"])
	}
	if len(g.seen()) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("the helper was launched for params that were refused")
	}
}

func TestUnboundItRefusesAndNamesWhy(t *testing.T) {
	h := newRunnerHarness(t, map[string]jobs.Handler{uninstall.JobType: uninstall.New(uninstall.Options{}).Run})
	h.deliver(t, uninstall.JobType, `{}`, "n-unbound")
	res := h.awaitResult(t)
	body, _ := res["result"].(map[string]any)
	if res["state"] != "refused" || body["reason"] == nil {
		t.Fatalf("an unbound capability must refuse with a reason, got %v", res)
	}
}

func TestAHelperThatDidNotStartIsAFailureNotAScheduledRemoval(t *testing.T) {
	f := &fakeLauncher{err: errors.New("systemd-run: not found")}
	h := newRunnerHarness(t, map[string]jobs.Handler{uninstall.JobType: bound(f).Run})
	h.deliver(t, uninstall.JobType, `{}`, "n-fail")
	if res := h.awaitResult(t); res["state"] != "failed" {
		t.Fatalf("state = %v, want failed: the server would otherwise read `succeeded` as "+
			"confirmation and revoke a certificate that is still installed", res["state"])
	}
}

func TestBindingStartsNothing(t *testing.T) {
	// Idle budget: registration is a map insert and Bind stores options. No
	// goroutine, no helper, nothing until a verified job arrives.
	before := runtime.NumGoroutine()
	f := &fakeLauncher{}
	svc := uninstall.New(uninstall.Options{})
	svc.Bind(uninstall.Options{Launch: f.launch, Binary: func() (string, error) { return "x", nil }, DataDir: "y"})
	if after := runtime.NumGoroutine(); after > before {
		t.Fatalf("Bind started %d goroutine(s)", after-before)
	}
	if len(f.seen()) != 0 || svc.Runs() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("Bind ran the capability")
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- harness
//
// THIS FILE CANNOT FORGE A jobs.VerifiedJob: the only way to hand the
// capability a job is to sign a real envelope and put it through the real
// runner, which is the same path a control plane's Reject takes.

type runnerHarness struct {
	runner *jobs.Runner
	sender *recordingSender
	priv   ed25519.PrivateKey
}

func newRunnerHarness(t *testing.T, registry map[string]jobs.Handler) *runnerHarness {
	t.Helper()
	pub, priv, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sender := &recordingSender{}
	r, err := jobs.New(jobs.Options{
		BaseContext: t.Context(),
		DeviceID:    testDeviceID,
		SigningKey:  pub,
		Nonces:      &permissiveNonces{seen: map[string]bool{}},
		Registry:    mapRegistry(registry),
		Sender:      sender,
		Logger:      quiet(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return &runnerHarness{runner: r, sender: sender, priv: priv}
}

func (h *runnerHarness) deliver(t *testing.T, jobType, params, nonce string) {
	t.Helper()
	now := time.Now().UTC()
	job := fmt.Sprintf(
		`{"id":%q,"type":%q,"device":%q,"params":%s,"issued_at":%q,"expires_at":%q,"nonce":%q}`,
		"9c3f0a11-0000-4000-8000-00000000c0de", jobType, testDeviceID, params,
		now.Add(-time.Second).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05Z"),
		now.Add(10*time.Minute).Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05Z"),
		nonce,
	)
	msg, err := canonical.BytesFromRaw(json.RawMessage(job))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("canonicalising the fixture failed: %v", err)
	}
	sig := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(ed25519.Sign(h.priv, msg))
	h.runner.Handle(context.Background(), []byte(fmt.Sprintf(`{"v":1,"kind":"job","job":%s,"sig":%q}`, job, sig)))
}

func (h *runnerHarness) awaitResult(t *testing.T) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for {
		for _, f := range h.sender.frames() {
			if f["kind"] == "job_result" {
				return f
			}
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatalf("no job_result within 5s; frames: %v", h.sender.frames())
		}
		time.Sleep(2 * time.Millisecond)
	}
}

type mapRegistry map[string]jobs.Handler

func (m mapRegistry) Lookup(jobType string) (jobs.Handler, bool) {
	h, ok := m[jobType]
	return h, ok
}

type permissiveNonces struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	seen map[string]bool
}

func (p *permissiveNonces) SeenOrRecord(nonce string, _ time.Time) bool {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()
	if p.seen[nonce] {
		return true
	}
	p.seen[nonce] = true
	return false
}

type recordingSender struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	sent []map[string]any
}

func (r *recordingSender) Send(_ context.Context, msg any) error {
	b, err := wire.Encode(msg)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var m map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &m); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.sent = append(r.sent, m)
	r.mu.Unlock()
	return nil
}

func (r *recordingSender) frames() []map[string]any {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]map[string]any(nil), r.sent...)
}
```

```go
package uninstall

import (
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"testing"
)

func TestTheCredentialsAreNamedAndAllFourAreThere(t *testing.T) {
	// The same four uninstall.ps1 and darwin/uninstall.sh remove BY NAME before
	// the recursive remove, so a partial failure still takes the private keys.
	want := []string{"enroll-token", "agent.key", "agent.key.pending", "previous.json"}
	for _, goos := range []string{"windows", "linux", "darwin"} {
		if got := For(goos, "b", "d").Credentials; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
			t.Fatalf("%s credentials = %v, want %v", goos, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestEachOSRemovesWhatItsInstallerPlaced(t *testing.T) {
	linux := For("linux", "/usr/local/bin/its-agent", "/var/lib/its-agent")
	if linux.ServiceName != "its-agent" ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(linux.Remove, []string{"/etc/systemd/system/its-agent.service", "/etc/its-agent"}) {
		t.Fatalf("linux targets = %+v", linux)
	}
	darwin := For("darwin", "/usr/local/libexec/its-agent", "/var/lib/its-agent")
	if darwin.ServiceName != "com.toeshee.itsagent" ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(darwin.Remove, []string{"/Library/LaunchDaemons/com.toeshee.itsagent.plist", "/var/log/its-agent"}) {
		t.Fatalf("darwin targets = %+v", darwin)
	}
	win := For("windows", `C:\Program Files\ITSuite\its-agent.exe`, `C:\ProgramData\ITSuite\agent`)
	if win.ServiceName != "its-agent" || len(win.Remove) != 0 ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(win.RemoveIfEmpty, []string{`C:\Program Files\ITSuite`}) {
		t.Fatalf("windows targets = %+v", win)
	}
}

func TestTheHelperRefusesTargetsThatCouldNotBeAnAgent(t *testing.T) {
	good := t.TempDir()
	cases := map[string]Targets{
		"relative data dir": {DataDir: "data", Binary: filepath.Join(good, "its-agent")},
		"root data dir":     {DataDir: string(filepath.Separator), Binary: filepath.Join(good, "its-agent")},
		"binary not ours":   {DataDir: good, Binary: filepath.Join(good, "bash")},
	}
	for name, tc := range cases {
		if err := checkTargets(tc); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: accepted %+v", name, tc)
		}
	}
	if err := checkTargets(Targets{DataDir: good, Binary: filepath.Join(good, "its-agent")}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("refused a real layout: %v", err)
	}
}
```

`agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall/helper_test.go`:

```go
package uninstall

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

type fakeSystem struct {
	calls   []string
	purged  bool
	stopErr error
}

func (f *fakeSystem) StopService(_ context.Context, name string) error {
	f.calls = append(f.calls, "stop "+name)
	return f.stopErr
}
func (f *fakeSystem) DeleteService(_ context.Context, name string) error {
	f.calls = append(f.calls, "delete "+name)
	return nil
}
func (f *fakeSystem) PurgePackage(context.Context) (bool, error) {
	f.calls = append(f.calls, "purge")
	return f.purged, nil
}
func (f *fakeSystem) ScheduleDelete(path string) error {
	f.calls = append(f.calls, "schedule "+filepath.Base(path))
	return nil
}
func (f *fakeSystem) HelperExiting(context.Context) { f.calls = append(f.calls, "exiting") }

// layout builds an installed agent in a temp dir: a binary, a data directory
// holding every credential plus one other file, and one extra path to remove.
func layout(t *testing.T) Targets {
	t.Helper()
	root := t.TempDir()
	data := filepath.Join(root, "data")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(data, "update"), 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, name := range []string{"enroll-token", "agent.key", "agent.key.pending", "previous.json", "identity.json"} {
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(data, name), []byte("x"), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	bin := filepath.Join(root, "bin", "its-agent")
	extra := filepath.Join(root, "etc-its-agent")
	for _, dir := range []string{filepath.Dir(bin), extra} {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(bin, []byte("elf"), 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return Targets{
		ServiceName:   "its-agent",
		Binary:        bin,
		DataDir:       data,
		Credentials:   []string{"enroll-token", "agent.key", "agent.key.pending", "previous.json"},
		Remove:        []string{extra},
		RemoveIfEmpty: []string{filepath.Dir(bin)},
	}
}

func gone(t *testing.T, paths ...string) {
	t.Helper()
	for _, p := range paths {
		if _, err := os.Stat(p); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			t.Errorf("%s survived the uninstall (%v)", p, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestTheHelperWaitsThenStopsThenRemovesEverything(t *testing.T) {
	tg := layout(t)
	sys := &fakeSystem{}
	var waited []time.Duration
	err := RunHelper(context.Background(), sys, tg, tg.Binary, func(d time.Duration) { waited = append(waited, d) })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("RunHelper: %v", err)
	}
	if len(waited) == 0 || waited[0] != HelperDelay {
		t.Fatalf("the first wait was %v, want %v: the job result must reach the gateway "+
			"before the service stops", waited, HelperDelay)
	}
	if want := []string{"stop its-agent", "purge", "delete its-agent", "exiting"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(sys.calls, want) {
		t.Fatalf("calls = %v, want %v", sys.calls, want)
	}
	gone(t, tg.DataDir, tg.Binary, tg.Remove[0], tg.RemoveIfEmpty[0])
}

func TestAPackageManagedAgentIsPurgedNotDeletedFileByFile(t *testing.T) {
	// dpkg owns /usr/bin/its-agent and the unit: deleting them by hand leaves
	// a package database that says they exist.
	tg := layout(t)
	sys := &fakeSystem{purged: true}
	if err := RunHelper(context.Background(), sys, tg, tg.Binary, func(time.Duration) {}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, c := range sys.calls {
		if strings.HasPrefix(c, "delete") {
			t.Fatalf("a purged package's service was deleted again: %v", sys.calls)
		}
	}
	// The data directory is not the package's, and still goes.
	gone(t, tg.DataDir)
}

func TestAStopFailureDoesNotLeaveTheCredentialsBehind(t *testing.T) {
	tg := layout(t)
	sys := &fakeSystem{stopErr: errors.New("service did not stop")}
	err := RunHelper(context.Background(), sys, tg, tg.Binary, func(time.Duration) {})
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "service did not stop") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v; the stop failure must be reported", err)
	}
	for _, name := range tg.Credentials {
		gone(t, filepath.Join(tg.DataDir, name))
	}
}

func TestAHelperRunningFromACopyAsksForItsOwnDeletionAtBoot(t *testing.T) {
	// Windows: the helper runs from a copy in the temp directory, because a
	// process cannot delete its own image. The copy goes at the next boot.
	tg := layout(t)
	sys := &fakeSystem{}
	self := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "its-agent-uninstall-1.exe")
	if err := RunHelper(context.Background(), sys, tg, self, func(time.Duration) {}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(sys.calls[len(sys.calls)-2:], []string{"schedule its-agent-uninstall-1.exe", "exiting"}) {
		t.Fatalf("calls = %v", sys.calls)
	}
}

func TestTheHelperRefusesToRunOnTargetsThatAreNotAnAgent(t *testing.T) {
	sys := &fakeSystem{}
	err := RunHelper(context.Background(), sys, Targets{DataDir: "/", Binary: "/bin/sh"}, "/bin/sh", func(time.Duration) {})
	if err == nil || len(sys.calls) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, calls = %v; nothing may be touched", err, sys.calls)
	}
}
```

```go
package uninstall_test

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"testing"

	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall"
)

// The exported schema is the contract (Task 9 generated it from the Pydantic
// models; backend/tests/test_wire_schema_export.py keeps it current). This is
// the Go-side guard, listed in internal/wire's notImplemented map.

const schemaDir = "../../../../docs/kernel/wire-protocol"

type jsonSchema struct {
	Title                string                     `json:"title"`
	Properties           map[string]json.RawMessage `json:"properties"`
	Required             []string                   `json:"required"`
	AdditionalProperties *bool                      `json:"additionalProperties"`
}

func load(t *testing.T, title string) jsonSchema {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(schemaDir, title+".schema.json"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the exported contract is missing: %v", err)
	}
	var s jsonSchema
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &s); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if s.Title != title {
		t.Fatalf("title = %q, want %q", s.Title, title)
	}
	return s
}

func tags(v any) []string {
	rt := reflect.TypeOf(v)
	out := []string{}
	for i := 0; i < rt.NumField(); i++ {
		tag := rt.Field(i).Tag.Get("json")
		for j := 0; j < len(tag); j++ {
			if tag[j] == ',' {
				tag = tag[:j]
				break
			}
		}
		out = append(out, tag)
	}
	return out
}

func TestTheParamsCarryNothing(t *testing.T) {
	s := load(t, "AgentUninstallParams")
	if len(s.Properties) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("AgentUninstallParams carries %v; a field here is a second input to a "+
			"command path that deletes files, and needs a review that knows it", s.Properties)
	}
	if s.AdditionalProperties == nil || *s.AdditionalProperties {
		t.Fatal("AgentUninstallParams is not extra=forbid server-side")
	}
	if got := tags(uninstall.ParamsShape{}); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("this package's params struct carries %v", got)
	}
}

func TestTheResultIsScheduled(t *testing.T) {
	s := load(t, "AgentUninstallResult")
	if _, ok := s.Properties["scheduled"]; !ok || len(s.Properties) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("AgentUninstallResult properties = %v", s.Properties)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(s.Required, []string{"scheduled"}) {
		t.Fatalf("required = %v", s.Required)
	}
	if got := tags(uninstall.ResultShape{}); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"scheduled"}) {
		t.Fatalf("this package's result struct carries %v", got)
	}
}
```

`agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall/uninstall.go`:

```go
// Package uninstall implements `agent.uninstall`: the agent removes itself
// (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-install-link-and-windows-push-design.md
// §1.5 -- the second half of rejecting a machine that joined through the
// install link).
//
// # A SIGNED JOB, and nothing else reaches it
//
// Reached only through internal/jobs, after the signature over the canonical
// bytes, the target device, freshness, the nonce and the type have all been
// checked. The params carry NOTHING: what to remove is this process's own
// knowledge -- its binary, its data directory, its service -- so there is no
// field a control plane could point at a path.
//
// # Scheduled, not done
//
// A process cannot stop and delete its own running service and then report.
// `Run` starts a DETACHED HELPER (this same binary with HelperArg, outside the
// service's process tree) and answers `{"scheduled": true}`. The helper waits
// HelperDelay so that answer reaches the gateway, then stops and deletes the
// service and removes the credentials, the data directory, the service
// definition and the binary (plan D7). The control plane reads `succeeded` as
// the agent's confirmation and revokes the certificate; a result lost with the
// data directory is covered by its 24-hour completion.
//
// # Dormancy
//
// Registration is a map insert and Bind stores options. No goroutine, no file
// and no process until a verified job arrives.
package uninstall

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/jobs"
)

// JobType matches `app/jobs/registry.py`'s registration.
const JobType = "agent.uninstall"

// HelperArg is the first argument main.go routes to HelperMain.
const HelperArg = "--uninstall-helper"

// EnvBinary tells the helper which installed binary to remove. The helper may
// be running from a copy (Windows), so it cannot use its own path.
const EnvBinary = "ITS_AGENT_UNINSTALL_BINARY"

// HelperDelay is how long the helper waits before stopping the service, so the
// job's `scheduled` answer is on the wire first.
const HelperDelay = 15 * time.Second

// params implements AgentUninstallParams.schema.json: no members. Unknown
// members are ignored (encoding/json's default) -- the inbound half of the
// wire-protocol rule.
type params struct{}

// result implements AgentUninstallResult.schema.json.
type result struct {
	Scheduled bool `json:"scheduled"`
}

type (
	// ParamsShape is `params`, exported for schema_test.go.
	ParamsShape = params
	// ResultShape is `result`, exported for schema_test.go.
	ResultShape = result
)

// Launcher starts the detached helper and returns once it is running. It
// never waits for the helper to finish.
type Launcher func(ctx context.Context, binary, dataDir string) error

// Options wires the capability. main.go binds LaunchHelper, os.Executable and
// the configured data directory.
type Options struct {
	Launch  Launcher
	Binary  func() (string, error)
	DataDir string
	Logger  *slog.Logger
}

// Service is the process-wide holder. The zero value refuses every job.
type Service struct {
	mu   sync.RWMutex
	opts Options
	runs atomic.Int64
}

// Process is the Service the default registry registers.
var Process = &Service{}

// New returns a Service with explicit wiring. Tests use it.
func New(o Options) *Service { return &Service{opts: o} }

// Bind stores the wiring. It starts nothing.
func (s *Service) Bind(o Options) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	s.opts = o
	s.mu.Unlock()
}

// Runs counts helpers this Service has started.
func (s *Service) Runs() int64 { return s.runs.Load() }

// Run is the capability.
func (s *Service) Run(ctx context.Context, job jobs.VerifiedJob) (any, error) {
	var p params
	if err := json.Unmarshal(job.Params(), &p); err != nil {
		return nil, jobs.Refuse("agent.uninstall params are not a JSON object")
	}
	s.mu.RLock()
	o := s.opts
	s.mu.RUnlock()
	if o.Launch == nil || o.Binary == nil || o.DataDir == "" {
		return nil, jobs.Refuse("agent.uninstall is registered but not bound in this process")
	}
	binary, err := o.Binary()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent.uninstall: cannot locate this agent's binary: %w", err)
	}
	if err := o.Launch(ctx, binary, o.DataDir); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent.uninstall: the removal helper did not start: %w", err)
	}
	s.runs.Add(1)
	log := o.Logger
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	log.Warn("agent.uninstall: this agent removes itself in 15 seconds", "job_id", job.ID())
	return result{Scheduled: true}, nil
}
```

`agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall/targets.go`:

```go
package uninstall

import (
	"fmt"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
)

// Targets is everything one installed agent consists of. It mirrors what the
// installers under agent/packaging/ place and what their uninstall scripts
// remove -- and nothing else.
type Targets struct {
	// ServiceName is the systemd unit / Windows service name, or the launchd label.
	ServiceName string
	// Binary is the INSTALLED binary (not the helper's own image).
	Binary string
	// DataDir holds the identity, the private key and the pinned anchors.
	DataDir string
	// Credentials are removed BY NAME before the recursive remove, so a
	// partial failure still takes the private keys (uninstall.ps1's rule).
	Credentials []string
	// Remove are service definitions, config and log directories.
	Remove []string
	// RemoveIfEmpty are directories removed only if nothing else is in them.
	RemoveIfEmpty []string
}

// For returns the targets for one OS. Pure, so every OS is testable here.
func For(goos, binary, dataDir string) Targets {
	t := Targets{
		ServiceName: "its-agent",
		Binary:      binary,
		DataDir:     dataDir,
		Credentials: []string{"enroll-token", "agent.key", "agent.key.pending", "previous.json"},
	}
	switch goos {
	case "windows":
		// install.ps1 / the self-installer: %ProgramFiles%\ITSuite\its-agent.exe.
		// The directory holds nothing else; it goes only if it is empty.
		t.RemoveIfEmpty = []string{winDir(binary)}
	case "darwin":
		t.ServiceName = "com.toeshee.itsagent"
		t.Remove = []string{"/Library/LaunchDaemons/com.toeshee.itsagent.plist", "/var/log/its-agent"}
	default:
		// linux/install.sh. A .deb-installed agent is purged by dpkg instead
		// (platformSystem.PurgePackage), which removes its own unit and binary.
		t.Remove = []string{"/etc/systemd/system/its-agent.service", "/etc/its-agent"}
	}
	return t
}

// winDir is filepath.Dir for a Windows path, on any GOOS (For is tested on Linux).
func winDir(p string) string {
	if i := strings.LastIndex(p, `\`); i > 0 {
		return p[:i]
	}
	return p
}

// checkTargets refuses targets that cannot be an installed agent. The helper
// deletes recursively; a data directory of "/" is a wiped machine.
func checkTargets(t Targets) error {
	dir := filepath.Clean(t.DataDir)
	if !filepath.IsAbs(dir) {
		return fmt.Errorf("uninstall: data directory %q is not absolute", t.DataDir)
	}
	if filepath.Dir(dir) == dir {
		return fmt.Errorf("uninstall: data directory %q is a filesystem root", t.DataDir)
	}
	base := strings.ToLower(filepath.Base(t.Binary))
	if base != "its-agent" && base != "its-agent.exe" {
		return fmt.Errorf("uninstall: %q is not an its-agent binary", t.Binary)
	}
	return nil
}
```

`agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall/helper.go`:

```go
package uninstall

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"time"

	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/config"
)

// System is what differs per service manager. platformSystem implements it
// in system_<goos>.go; tests use a fake.
type System interface {
	StopService(ctx context.Context, name string) error
	DeleteService(ctx context.Context, name string) error
	// PurgePackage removes a package-manager-installed agent and reports
	// whether there was one.
	PurgePackage(ctx context.Context) (bool, error)
	// ScheduleDelete removes a file at the next boot (the helper's own copy on
	// Windows, which a running process cannot delete).
	ScheduleDelete(path string) error
	// HelperExiting is the helper's last act (daemon-reload, or unloading its
	// own launchd job).
	HelperExiting(ctx context.Context)
}

// RunHelper removes the agent. Every step runs even if an earlier one failed;
// the failures are joined. `self` is the helper's own image.
func RunHelper(ctx context.Context, sys System, t Targets, self string, wait func(time.Duration)) error {
	if err := checkTargets(t); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	wait(HelperDelay)
	var errs []error
	note := func(err error) {
		if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
			errs = append(errs, err)
		}
	}
	note(sys.StopService(ctx, t.ServiceName))
	purged, err := sys.PurgePackage(ctx)
	note(err)
	if !purged {
		note(sys.DeleteService(ctx, t.ServiceName))
	}
	for _, name := range t.Credentials {
		note(os.Remove(filepath.Join(t.DataDir, name)))
	}
	note(os.RemoveAll(t.DataDir))
	for _, p := range t.Remove {
		note(os.RemoveAll(p))
	}
	if !purged {
		// Windows keeps an image mapped briefly after its process exits.
		var rmErr error
		for attempt := 0; attempt < 20; attempt++ {
			if rmErr = os.Remove(t.Binary); rmErr == nil || errors.Is(rmErr, os.ErrNotExist) {
				rmErr = nil
				break
			}
			wait(500 * time.Millisecond)
		}
		note(rmErr)
	}
	for _, dir := range t.RemoveIfEmpty {
		_ = os.Remove(dir) // fails, correctly, if anything else is in it
	}
	if self != "" && self != t.Binary {
		note(sys.ScheduleDelete(self))
	}
	sys.HelperExiting(ctx)
	return errors.Join(errs...)
}

// HelperMain is `its-agent --uninstall-helper`. Its inputs are the environment
// the capability set when it launched this process -- never a job's params.
func HelperMain() int {
	log := slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(os.Stderr, &slog.HandlerOptions{Level: slog.LevelInfo}))
	binary, dataDir := os.Getenv(EnvBinary), os.Getenv(config.EnvDataDir)
	if binary == "" || dataDir == "" {
		log.Error("uninstall helper: started without its environment; nothing removed")
		return 2
	}
	self, _ := os.Executable()
	if err := RunHelper(context.Background(), platformSystem{}, For(runtime.GOOS, binary, dataDir), self, time.Sleep); err != nil {
		log.Error("uninstall helper finished with errors", "err", err)
		return 1
	}
	log.Info("its-agent removed")
	return 0
}
```

`agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall/system_linux.go`:

```go
//go:build linux

package uninstall

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"time"

	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/config"
)

// Supported: this build advertises agent.uninstall.
const Supported = true

// helperUnit is a transient unit OUTSIDE its-agent.service's cgroup, which
// KillMode=control-group would otherwise kill the moment the service stops --
// internal/update's self-update helper is launched the same way.
const helperUnit = "its-agent-uninstall"

// LaunchHelper starts `<binary> --uninstall-helper` as a transient unit.
func LaunchHelper(ctx context.Context, binary, dataDir string) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	args := []string{
		"--unit=" + helperUnit, "--collect", "--no-block", "--quiet",
		"-p", "Type=exec",
		"--setenv=" + config.EnvDataDir + "=" + dataDir,
		"--setenv=" + EnvBinary + "=" + binary,
		"--", binary, HelperArg,
	}
	if out, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "systemd-run", args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("systemd-run: %w: %s", err, out)
	}
	return nil
}

type platformSystem struct{}

func systemctl(ctx context.Context, args ...string) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 90*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if out, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "systemctl", args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("systemctl %v: %w: %s", args, err, out)
	}
	return nil
}

func (platformSystem) StopService(ctx context.Context, name string) error {
	return systemctl(ctx, "stop", name+".service")
}

func (platformSystem) DeleteService(ctx context.Context, name string) error {
	return systemctl(ctx, "disable", name+".service")
}

// PurgePackage: a .deb-installed agent (agent/packaging/linux/build-deb.sh)
// is removed by dpkg, which owns /usr/bin/its-agent and the packaged unit.
func (platformSystem) PurgePackage(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) {
	if _, err := os.Stat("/var/lib/dpkg/info/its-agent.list"); err != nil {
		return false, nil
	}
	if out, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "dpkg", "--purge", "its-agent").CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
		return true, fmt.Errorf("dpkg --purge its-agent: %w: %s", err, out)
	}
	return true, nil
}

func (platformSystem) ScheduleDelete(string) error { return nil }

func (platformSystem) HelperExiting(ctx context.Context) {
	_ = systemctl(ctx, "daemon-reload")
	_ = systemctl(ctx, "reset-failed", "its-agent.service")
}
```

`agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall/system_darwin.go`:

```go
//go:build darwin

package uninstall

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"os/exec"
	"time"

	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/config"
)

// Supported: this build advertises agent.uninstall.
const Supported = true

// helperLabel is a launchd job of its own: launchd kills a job's children
// when it boots the job out, so the helper cannot be the service's child.
const helperLabel = "com.toeshee.itsagent.uninstall"

// LaunchHelper submits `<binary> --uninstall-helper` as its own launchd job.
// `/usr/bin/env` carries the two variables; `launchctl submit` takes no
// environment of its own.
func LaunchHelper(ctx context.Context, binary, dataDir string) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	_ = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "launchctl", "remove", helperLabel).Run()
	out, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "launchctl", "submit", "-l", helperLabel, "--",
		"/usr/bin/env", config.EnvDataDir+"="+dataDir, EnvBinary+"="+binary, binary, HelperArg,
	).CombinedOutput()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("launchctl submit: %w: %s", err, out)
	}
	return nil
}

type platformSystem struct{}

func (platformSystem) StopService(ctx context.Context, name string) error {
	if out, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "launchctl", "bootout", "system/"+name).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("launchctl bootout system/%s: %w: %s", name, err, out)
	}
	return nil
}

// DeleteService: the plist is in Targets.Remove; bootout already unloaded it.
func (platformSystem) DeleteService(context.Context, string) error { return nil }

func (platformSystem) PurgePackage(context.Context) (bool, error) { return false, nil }

func (platformSystem) ScheduleDelete(string) error { return nil }

// HelperExiting removes the helper's own submitted job, which launchd would
// otherwise restart. Not waited for: it signals this very process.
func (platformSystem) HelperExiting(context.Context) {
	_ = exec.Command("launchctl", "remove", helperLabel).Start()
}
```

`agent/internal/capabilities/uninstall/system_windows.go`:

```go
//go:build windows

package uninstall

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"golang.org/x/sys/windows"
	"golang.org/x/sys/windows/svc"
	"golang.org/x/sys/windows/svc/mgr"

	"github.com/toeshee/it-suite/agent/internal/config"
)

// Supported: this build advertises agent.uninstall.
const Supported = true

// LaunchHelper copies the binary to the system temp directory and starts the
// COPY detached: a running image cannot be deleted, and the helper must delete
// the installed one. The SCM does not kill a service's children on stop;
// breakaway is retried without if the job object denies it (the self-update
// helper's pattern, internal/update/service_windows.go).
func LaunchHelper(_ context.Context, binary, dataDir string) error {
	copyPath := filepath.Join(os.TempDir(), fmt.Sprintf("its-agent-uninstall-%d.exe", time.Now().UnixNano()))
	if err := copyFile(binary, copyPath); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("copy the helper: %w", err)
	}
	start := func(flags uint32) error {
		cmd := exec.Command(copyPath, HelperArg)
		cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), config.EnvDataDir+"="+dataDir, EnvBinary+"="+binary)
		cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{CreationFlags: flags, HideWindow: true}
		if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return cmd.Process.Release()
	}
	base := uint32(windows.DETACHED_PROCESS | windows.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP)
	if err := start(base | windows.CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB); err == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return start(base)
}

func copyFile(src, dst string) error {
	in, err := os.Open(src)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer in.Close()
	out, err := os.OpenFile(dst, os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL|os.O_WRONLY, 0o700)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := io.Copy(out, in); err != nil {
		out.Close()
		return err
	}
	return out.Close()
}

type platformSystem struct{}

func open(name string) (*mgr.Mgr, *mgr.Service, error) {
	m, err := mgr.Connect()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	s, err := m.OpenService(name)
	if err != nil {
		m.Disconnect()
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return m, s, nil
}

func (platformSystem) StopService(_ context.Context, name string) error {
	m, s, err := open(name)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer m.Disconnect()
	defer s.Close()
	if _, err := s.Control(svc.Stop); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_SERVICE_NOT_ACTIVE) {
		return err
	}
	deadline := time.Now().Add(60 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		st, err := s.Query()
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if st.State == svc.Stopped {
			return nil
		}
		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("the %s service did not stop within 60s", name)
}

func (platformSystem) DeleteService(_ context.Context, name string) error {
	m, s, err := open(name)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer m.Disconnect()
	defer s.Close()
	return s.Delete()
}

func (platformSystem) PurgePackage(context.Context) (bool, error) { return false, nil }

// ScheduleDelete asks Windows to delete the helper's own copy at the next boot.
func (platformSystem) ScheduleDelete(path string) error {
	p, err := windows.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return windows.MoveFileEx(p, nil, windows.MOVEFILE_DELAY_UNTIL_REBOOT)
}

func (platformSystem) HelperExiting(context.Context) {}
```

```go
var errUnsupported = errors.New("agent.uninstall is not supported on this operating system")
```

### Task 11: Agent — a link code in the token file enrolls with `link_code`

**Files:**
- Modify: `agent/internal/enroll/enroll.go` (`enrollRequest`, `Enroll`, new `IsLinkCode`)
- Test: `agent/internal/enroll/link_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the server contract from Task 6: `POST /enroll/v1` accepts exactly one of `token` or `link_code`; a link code is 32 characters of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` (Task 3's `looks_like_code`).
- Produces: `enroll.IsLinkCode(s string) bool`. `Enroll` sends `{"link_code": <code>}` and no `token` member when the credential it was given (env or token file) is link-code shaped; otherwise it sends `{"token": ...}` and no `link_code` member, exactly as before. Task 12's self-installer and Task 7's `install.sh` both put the code in the ordinary token file and rely on this.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`agent/internal/enroll/link_test.go`:

```go
package enroll

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
)

// The install link (spec 2026-09-28 §1.4) reuses the installer's token file:
// the self-installer and install.sh write the link code where a token would
// go, and the agent tells the two apart by SHAPE. A token is
// `<uuid>.<secret>` and always has a dot; a link code is 32 URL-safe
// characters and never does.

const linkCode = "AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012_-9"

func TestIsLinkCode(t *testing.T) {
	for s, want := range map[string]bool{
		linkCode:                                  true,
		"0192f000-0000-7000-8000-00000000e2e0.s3c": false, // a token
		linkCode[:31]:                             false,
		linkCode + "A":                            false,
		"AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012_-.":        false,
		"":                                        false,
	} {
		if got := IsLinkCode(s); got != want {
			t.Errorf("IsLinkCode(%q) = %v, want %v", s, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestALinkCodeInTheTokenFileIsSentAsLinkCodeAndNeverAsAToken(t *testing.T) {
	cp := newFakeCP(t)
	cfg := cfgWithTokenFile(t, cp, linkCode)
	if _, err := LoadOrEnroll(context.Background(), cfg, Options{Capabilities: []string{"agent.uninstall"}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("LoadOrEnroll: %v", err)
	}
	if cp.lastBody["link_code"] != linkCode {
		t.Fatalf("link_code = %v, want the code", cp.lastBody["link_code"])
	}
	if _, present := cp.lastBody["token"]; present {
		t.Fatalf("the body carries a token member too (%v); the server refuses a body with both", cp.lastBody)
	}
	caps, _ := cp.lastBody["capabilities"].(map[string]any)
	if caps["agent.uninstall"] != true {
		t.Fatalf("capabilities = %v; the review needs to know this agent can uninstall itself", caps)
	}
}

func TestATokenIsStillSentAsATokenAndCarriesNoLinkCode(t *testing.T) {
	cp := newFakeCP(t)
	cfg := cfgWithTokenFile(t, cp, "0192f000-0000-7000-8000-00000000e2e0.s3cr3t")
	if _, err := LoadOrEnroll(context.Background(), cfg, Options{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("LoadOrEnroll: %v", err)
	}
	if cp.lastBody["token"] != "0192f000-0000-7000-8000-00000000e2e0.s3cr3t" {
		t.Fatalf("token = %v", cp.lastBody["token"])
	}
	if _, present := cp.lastBody["link_code"]; present {
		t.Fatal("a token enrollment carries a link_code member; every control plane before this " +
			"one ignores it, but it must not be sent at all")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Run: `cd agent && go test ./internal/enroll/ -run 'LinkCode|StillSentAsAToken' -count=1`
Expected: FAIL — `undefined: IsLinkCode`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`agent/internal/enroll/enroll.go`, `enrollRequest` becomes:

```go
type enrollRequest struct {
	// Exactly ONE of these is set. `omitempty` on `token` is safe for every
	// older control plane: Enroll refuses an empty credential before building
	// the body, so a token enrollment always carries a non-empty token.
	Token string `json:"token,omitempty"`
	// LinkCode is the install link's code (spec 2026-09-28 §1.4). ADDITIVE:
	// a control plane older than the install link answers 422 (no token),
	// which this agent reports as a rejection and keeps the file for.
	LinkCode     string          `json:"link_code,omitempty"`
	CSRPEM       string          `json:"csr_pem"`
	DeviceFacts  deviceFacts     `json:"device_facts"`
	Capabilities map[string]bool `json:"capabilities"`
}
```

Add, after `enrollResponse`:

```go
// linkCodeLength is the install link code's length (Task 3: 32 characters of
// secrets.token_urlsafe(24)).
const linkCodeLength = 32

// IsLinkCode reports whether a credential is an install-link code rather than
// an enrollment token. A token is `<uuid>.<secret>` and always carries a dot;
// a link code is 32 characters of [A-Za-z0-9_-] and never does, so the shape
// decides without a second file or a flag -- and the code still reaches the
// agent only through the token file, never argv.
func IsLinkCode(s string) bool {
	if len(s) != linkCodeLength {
		return false
	}
	for _, c := range s {
		switch {
		case c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z', c >= 'a' && c <= 'z', c >= '0' && c <= '9', c == '-', c == '_':
		default:
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}
```

In `Enroll`, the body construction becomes:

```go
	request := enrollRequest{
		CSRPEM:       string(csrPEM),
		DeviceFacts:  facts,
		Capabilities: caps,
	}
	if IsLinkCode(cfg.EnrollToken) {
		request.LinkCode = cfg.EnrollToken
	} else {
		request.Token = cfg.EnrollToken
	}
	body, err := json.Marshal(request)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
```

and the log line directly above the POST gains the path it took, never the value: `log.Info("enrolling", "url", url, "hostname", facts.Hostname, "os_family", facts.OSFamily, "via_install_link", request.LinkCode != "")`.

Run: `cd agent && go test ./internal/enroll/ -count=1 && go vet ./internal/enroll/`
Expected: `ok` — the existing token tests (including `TestASuccessfulEnrollmentDeletesTheInstallersTokenFile`) still pass, because the spent-file deletion is unchanged for both credentials.

```bash
git commit -m "agent: a link code in the token file enrolls through link_code"
```

### Task 12: Agent — the Windows self-installer (`agent/internal/install`)

> **Rewritten 2026-09-28 to match the code as built** (review I9). Two things changed it after the first draft: the operator's decision **D6a** (the console is in the file name; the Mark of the Web is not read, `motw.go` does not exist) and the review of Tasks 10–13 (I1–I4, I6, I8). The code under `agent/internal/install/` is the source of truth; this task records what each file is for, what each test pins, and why.

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/internal/install/mode.go` — `FilePrefix`, `ElevatedArg`, `EnvNoUI` (the constants `cmd/agent` reads without calling in) and `confirmText` (the host question, I3)
- Create: `agent/internal/install/name.go` — `Link{Console, Code}` and `FromFileName` (D6a)
- Create: `agent/internal/install/plan.go` — what install.ps1 does, as data (`Plan`, `NewPlan`)
- Create: `agent/internal/install/verify.go` — `ReleaseMeta`, `fetchReleaseMeta`, `metaClient`, `verifyStaged` (I1)
- Create: `agent/internal/install/install_windows.go` — `Run`, `prelude`, `confirmHost`, `install`, `openShareReadOnly`, `relaunchElevated`, `apply`, `checkOwner`, `stage`, `verifyFile`, `renameWithRetry`, `report`; `var shellExecute`, `var messageBox`
- Create: `agent/internal/install/install_other.go` — `Run` says "Windows only" and exits 2
- Test: `name_test.go`, `plan_test.go`, `dormant_test.go`, `verify_test.go`, `prompt_test.go`, `imports_test.go` (portable); `install_windows_test.go` (runs in the `agent-windows` CI job)
- Create: `agent/internal/secdir` `RestrictOwned`, `MkdirOwned` (+ `owned_windows_test.go`) — I4, M1
- Modify: `agent/cmd/agent/main.go` (`installerRequested`, the branch in `main`), `agent/cmd/agent/main_test.go`
- Modify: `agent/packaging/windows/install.ps1`, `its-agent-enroll.ps1` (owner, `CreateNew`), `agent/packaging/owner_test.go`, `packaging_test.go` (I4)
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (`GOOS=windows go test -c -o /dev/null ./internal/install`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `secdir.RestrictOwned(dir) error` (owner `BA` + `D:PAI(A;OICI;FA;;;SY)(A;OICI;FA;;;BA)`); `update.ReleaseKey()`, `update.CheckReleaseSignature(pub, version, "windows", goarch, sha256hex, sigB64)` — the self-update check, unchanged; `golang.org/x/sys/windows` (`SetDefaultDllDirectories`, `CreateFile`, `ShellExecute`, `MessageBox`, `GetCurrentProcessToken().IsElevated()`, `GetNamedSecurityInfo`), `windows/svc/mgr`, `windows/registry`; Task 11's `link_code` enrollment; from the backend, the download name `ITMS-Agent-Setup_<host>[@<port>]_<code>.exe` (Task 7, D6a) and **`GET /i/<code>/windows.sig?arch=<amd64|arm64>` → `{"version","sha256","signature"}`** under the link's 404 rules (added by the backend for I1).
- Produces (package `install`): `FilePrefix = "ITMS-Agent-Setup_"`, `ElevatedArg = "--elevated-install"`, `EnvNoUI = "ITS_AGENT_INSTALLER_NO_UI"`; `type Link struct{ Console, Code string }`; `FromFileName(path string) (Link, bool)`; `Plan`, `NewPlan(programFiles, programData, console string) Plan`; `ReleaseMeta`; `Run(args []string, exe string, link Link) int`. In `cmd/agent`: `installerRequested(args []string, exe string) bool`.
- **No new dependency.** Everything is `golang.org/x/sys` v0.33.0 and the standard library, and `imports_test.go` proves the service already links all of it.

**Decisions recorded here.**
- **D6a (operator).** The console is `https://<host>[:<port>]` from the file name, verified against the system trust store; nothing reads `Zone.Identifier`.
- **D11 — the file name is argv[0].** The code is never an *argument*: not a flag, not in the UAC re-launch (`--elevated-install` only), not in the service definition or its environment. It *is* in the installer's image path while it runs, so process-creation logging records it — Security event 4688 (with command-line auditing), Sysmon event 1, and whatever an EDR/SIEM forwards. The packaging README lists this beside the Downloads-folder residual (I8).
- **The host is unauthenticated (I3).** A file name is writable by whoever hands the technician the file, so a Yes/No question names the host before anything is elevated.
- **The installed bytes are verified (I1).** Only the published, signed release becomes the SYSTEM service. With no pinned release key (a development build) the signature is `not_required`, as for self-update; the digest is still checked.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests (portable)**

- `name_test.go` — `FromFileName` accepts `ITMS-Agent-Setup_itms.toeshee.com_<32>.exe`, the same with ` (1)` and `(12)`, `.EXE`/`.Exe`, `@8443`, `@1`, `@65535`, `localhost@<port>`, a hyphenated mixed-case host, and codes that begin with `_` or `-` (the code is read from the right); it refuses the installed service's own name, a missing code, 31- and 33-character codes, a code outside the alphabet, the pre-D6a shape (code only), an IPv4 literal and `10.1.2`, an underscore, a leading/trailing `-`, empty labels, a 64-character label, a 254-character host (253 is accepted), `user@host`, `host:port`, ports `0`, `70000`, `65536`, empty, `08443`, non-digits and two `@`, a non-browser suffix, `.exe.crdownload`, and the wrong-case prefix.
- `plan_test.go` — `NewPlan` is install.ps1's layout (`%ProgramFiles%\ITSuite\its-agent.exe`, `%ProgramData%\ITSuite\agent`, the service name/display name/description, environment = data dir + enroll URL and never the code, reset 300 / 5 s, 15 s, 60 s) and `TestThePlanAgreesWithInstallPs1` reads the script so the two cannot drift.
- `dormant_test.go` — no `init()` and no package-level variable initialised by a call in any non-test file (function values such as `shellExecute = windows.ShellExecute` are not calls).
- `verify_test.go` — `verifyStaged` accepts the signed release (and an upper-case digest); refuses swapped bytes (`SHA-256`), a digest the key did not sign (`signature_invalid`), no signature, a non-base64 signature, a relabelled version, another key's signature, and an arm64 release on amd64. `fetchReleaseMeta` requests `/i/<code>/windows.sig?arch=<arch>`, ignores additive members, and refuses a 404, a redirect, non-JSON, a missing version, a short or non-hex digest, and an oversized body.
- `prompt_test.go` — `confirmText` is `Install the ITMS agent and connect this computer to <host[:port]>?`.
- `imports_test.go` (I6) — `TestInstallImportsArePinned` pins the direct imports under `GOOS=windows` to an explicit list; `TestInstallLinksNothingTheServiceDoesNotAlreadyLink` requires `go list -deps ./internal/install` ⊆ `go list -deps` of `cmd/agent`'s other imports. (It found `windows/registry` looked new until `internal/capabilities/inventory` was counted: the service does link it.)
- `cmd/agent/main_test.go` — `installerRequested` is true only for no arguments or exactly `--elevated-install` AND the prefix; the code as an argument, `--version`, and the installed service's own name are not installer mode.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests (Windows, run by the `agent-windows` job)**

`install_windows_test.go` replaces `shellExecute` and `messageBox` and asserts: the re-launch is `ShellExecute(0, "runas", <this file>, "--elevated-install", nil, SW_SHOWNORMAL)`; a **No** to the host question returns 1 and never calls `ShellExecute`; **Yes** relaunches and returns 0; the elevated copy (`--elevated-install`, elevated) is not asked again and proceeds; an already-elevated double-click is still asked; `EnvNoUI` asks nothing; an unelevated `--elevated-install` stops with exit 1 instead of looping. `secdir/owned_windows_test.go` reads back `RestrictOwned`'s and `MkdirOwned`'s owner (BA) and protected DACL, and refuses `MkdirOwned` over an existing directory.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the portable half** — `mode.go`, `name.go`, `plan.go`, `verify.go`, `install_other.go` as in the tree. `FromFileName`: base name → strip `.exe` (any case) → strip one ` (n)`/`(n)` → require `FilePrefix` → the last 32 characters are the code and the one before must be `_` → the rest is `host[@port]`, validated (`validHost`: labels `[A-Za-z0-9-]` 1–63, not starting/ending with `-`, ≤ 253, last label not all digits; `validPort`: 1–65535, no leading zero). `verifyStaged`: SHA-256 of the staged reader must equal `meta.SHA256` (case-insensitive), then `update.CheckReleaseSignature(pub, meta.Version, "windows", goarch, sum, meta.Signature)` must return no refusal. `metaClient`: 30 s timeout, `CheckRedirect` → `http.ErrUseLastResponse`, system roots.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the Windows half** — `install_windows.go`:
  1. `Run`: **first statement** `windows.SetDefaultDllDirectories(LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32)`, exit 1 on error (I2); then `prelude`, then `install`.
  2. `prelude`: in the process the person started (no arguments), ask `confirmHost` (`MB_YESNO|MB_ICONQUESTION|MB_DEFBUTTON2`) unless `EnvNoUI=1`; No → exit 1. Elevated → proceed. Unelevated `--elevated-install` → report and exit 1. Otherwise `relaunchElevated` and exit 0 (`ERROR_CANCELLED` → the "cancelled" message).
  3. `install` (elevated): `openShareReadOnly(exe)` — `CreateFile(GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, OPEN_EXISTING)`, held for the rest of the process; `fetchReleaseMeta(ctx, metaClient(nil), link, runtime.GOARCH)`; `update.ReleaseKey()`; `apply`. Any verification failure reports *"This file could not be confirmed as the ITMS agent the install link serves, so nothing was installed…"*.
  4. `apply`: on a **fresh** install (no `its-agent` service), `checkOwner` refuses an existing `%ProgramData%\ITSuite` or data directory owned by anyone but Administrators, SYSTEM or the installing admin (a pre-created directory would carry planted identity files past the ACL); create directories; `secdir.RestrictOwned(DataDir)`; `stage` from the held handle into `%ProgramFiles%\ITSuite\.its-agent.exe.new` (`O_EXCL`); `verifyFile` reads the **staged** bytes back through `verifyStaged` — on failure remove the staged file and stop, before the token or the service is touched; remove any `enroll-token` and create it `O_EXCL` with the code (no BOM, no newline); stop an existing service; `renameWithRetry` (20 × 500 ms) onto the target; `CreateService` with no arguments (`StartAutomatic`, `ErrorNormal`, display name, description); the `Environment` MultiString; recovery actions; start.
  5. `report` writes stderr and, unless `EnvNoUI=1`, shows the result window via `messageBox`.

`cmd/agent/main.go`: `installerRequested` (no call into the package); in `main()`, after the two helper branches: `if exe, err := os.Executable(); err == nil && installerRequested(os.Args[1:], exe) { if link, ok := install.FromFileName(exe); ok { os.Exit(install.Run(os.Args[1:], exe, link)) } }`.

`install.ps1` and `its-agent-enroll.ps1`: `$acl.SetOwner((New-Object System.Security.Principal.NTAccount('BUILTIN\Administrators')))` before `Set-Acl`; `Remove-Item -LiteralPath $tokenFile -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue` then `[System.IO.File]::Open($tokenFile, [System.IO.FileMode]::CreateNew, …)` instead of `WriteAllText`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Verify** — from `agent/`: `go test ./internal/install/ ./internal/secdir/ ./packaging/ ./cmd/agent/ -count=1`; `go vet ./...`; `GOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64 go vet ./...`; `GOOS=windows GOARCH=arm64 go vet ./...`; `GOOS=darwin go vet ./...`; `GOOS=windows go test -c -o /dev/null ./internal/install`; `GOOS=windows go test -c -o /dev/null ./internal/secdir`; `staticcheck ./...` and `GOOS=windows staticcheck ./internal/install/ ./internal/secdir/ ./cmd/agent/`; the ci.yml idle-budget step. The budget benchmarks are **not** evidence for this package — they measure the running service; `imports_test.go` and `dormant_test.go` are.

`frontend/src/modules/agent-releases/InstallLinkPanel.test.tsx`:

```tsx
/**
 * The install link's settings (install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.1, §1.6).
 *
 * What is pinned: the link cannot be switched on with no network; the full
 * link is shown ONCE, from the response that minted it, and is gone when the
 * operator says they have it; regenerating asks first; the panel says how long
 * the link has been on and how many computers joined through it.
 */

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { ApiError } from "../../lib/api";
import type { ApiClient, InstallLinkSaved, InstallLinkSettings } from "../../lib/api";
import { InstallLinkPanel, fromLocalInput, parseNetworks, toLocalInput } from "./InstallLinkPanel";

const mockUseApi = vi.fn();
vi.mock("../../lib/ApiContext", () => ({ useApi: () => mockUseApi() }));

const OFF: InstallLinkSettings = {
  enabled: false, has_code: false, networks: [], disable_at: null,
  hourly_limit: 50, enabled_at: null, joined_since_enabled: 0,
};
const LINK = "https://itms.example.com/i/AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012_-9";

function renderPanel(api: Partial<ApiClient>) {
  mockUseApi.mockReturnValue(api as ApiClient);
  const queryClient = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <InstallLinkPanel zone="Europe/Lisbon" />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => mockUseApi.mockReset());

describe("InstallLinkPanel", () => {
  it("will not switch the link on until a network is listed", async () => {
    const saveInstallLink = vi.fn();
    renderPanel({ getInstallLink: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(OFF), saveInstallLink });
    expect(await screen.findByText(/No link has been generated yet/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "Accept new computers through the link" }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("List at least one network");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save install link" })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(saveInstallLink).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("switching on sends the networks and shows the new link exactly once", async () => {
    const saved: InstallLinkSaved = {
      ...OFF, enabled: true, has_code: true, networks: ["10.20.0.0/16", "fd00::/8"],
      enabled_at: "2026-09-28T08:00:00Z", url: LINK,
    };
    const saveInstallLink = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(saved);
    renderPanel({ getInstallLink: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(OFF), saveInstallLink });
    await userEvent.type(
      await screen.findByLabelText("Networks the link answers"),
      "10.20.0.0/16{enter}{enter}fd00::/8",
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "Accept new computers through the link" }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save install link" }));

    expect(saveInstallLink).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      enabled: true, networks: ["10.20.0.0/16", "fd00::/8"], disable_at: null, hourly_limit: 50,
    });
    const reveal = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "The new install link" });
    expect(within(reveal).getByText(LINK)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(reveal).toHaveTextContent("shown once");

    await userEvent.click(within(reveal).getByRole("button", { name: "I have copied it" }));
    expect(screen.queryByText(LINK)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("says how long the link has been on and how many computers joined", async () => {
    renderPanel({
      getInstallLink: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ...OFF, enabled: true, has_code: true, networks: ["10.20.0.0/16"],
        enabled_at: "2026-09-27T08:00:00Z", joined_since_enabled: 3,
        disable_at: "2026-10-03T18:00:00Z",
      }),
    });
    const status = await screen.findByText(/On since/);
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent("3 computers joined since then");
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent("switches itself off");
    expect(screen.getByText(/shown once, when it was generated/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("asks before generating a new link, then shows it once", async () => {
    const regenerateInstallLink = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ...OFF, enabled: true, has_code: true, networks: ["10.20.0.0/16"],
      enabled_at: "2026-09-27T08:00:00Z", url: LINK,
    });
    renderPanel({
      getInstallLink: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ...OFF, enabled: true, has_code: true, networks: ["10.20.0.0/16"], enabled_at: "2026-09-27T08:00:00Z",
      }),
      regenerateInstallLink,
    });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Generate a new link…" }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Generate a new install link" });
    expect(dialog).toHaveTextContent("stops working at once");
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Cancel" }));
    expect(regenerateInstallLink).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Generate a new link…" }));
    await userEvent.click(
      within(screen.getByRole("dialog")).getByRole("button", { name: "Generate new link" }),
    );
    expect(regenerateInstallLink).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(await screen.findByText(LINK)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("a refused save says why, in the server's words", async () => {
    renderPanel({
      getInstallLink: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(OFF),
      saveInstallLink: vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(
        new ApiError(422, JSON.stringify({ detail: "'10.0.0.1/24' is not a network (has host bits set)" })),
      ),
    });
    await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText("Networks the link answers"), "10.0.0.1/24");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save install link" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("is not a network");
  });
});

describe("the form's conversions", () => {
  it("reads one network per line or comma and drops blanks", () => {
    expect(parseNetworks(" 10.0.0.0/8 \n\n fd00::/8, 192.168.1.0/24 ")).toEqual([
      "10.0.0.0/8", "fd00::/8", "192.168.1.0/24",
    ]);
  });

  it("round-trips the switch-off time through the browser's local input", () => {
    const iso = "2026-10-03T18:30:00.000Z";
    expect(fromLocalInput(toLocalInput(iso))).toBe(iso);
    expect(toLocalInput(null)).toBe("");
    expect(fromLocalInput("")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/lib/api.ts`, after `AgentInstallCommand`:

```ts
/**
 * The organisation's install link (install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.1).
 *
 * THE LINK ITSELF IS NOT HERE. It exists server-side only as a hash; the full
 * link is in `InstallLinkSaved.url`, in the one response that generated it.
 */
export interface InstallLinkSettings {
  /** On right now: switched on and not past its automatic switch-off. */
  enabled: boolean;
  /** Whether a link has ever been generated. */
  has_code: boolean;
  networks: string[];
  disable_at: string | null;
  hourly_limit: number;
  /** When it was switched on, while it is on. */
  enabled_at: string | null;
  joined_since_enabled: number;
}

export interface InstallLinkSaved extends InstallLinkSettings {
  /** The full link, present ONLY in the response that generated it. */
  url: string | null;
}

export interface InstallLinkUpdate {
  enabled: boolean;
  networks: string[];
  disable_at: string | null;
  hourly_limit: number;
}
```

and methods, after `getAgentDeploymentPackage`:

```ts
  /** `enrollment.link.manage`, on every verb including this read. */
  getInstallLink(): Promise<InstallLinkSettings> {
    return this.request<InstallLinkSettings>("/install-link");
  }

  saveInstallLink(body: InstallLinkUpdate): Promise<InstallLinkSaved> {
    return this.request<InstallLinkSaved>("/install-link", {
      method: "PUT",
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  }

  /** A new code: the old link stops working the moment this returns. */
  regenerateInstallLink(): Promise<InstallLinkSaved> {
    return this.request<InstallLinkSaved>("/install-link/regenerate", { method: "POST" });
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The panel**

`frontend/src/modules/agent-releases/InstallLinkPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
/**
 * The install link (install-link spec 2026-09-28 §1.1, §1.6).
 *
 * ONE LINK FOR EVERY COMPUTER A TECHNICIAN SETS UP, off by default. What
 * bounds it is on this panel: the networks it answers, an hourly limit, an
 * optional automatic switch-off, and regeneration. Machines that join wait
 * for review (Devices → Waiting for review) and receive nothing but
 * inventory, health monitoring and certificate renewal until someone approves
 * them.
 *
 * THE LINK IS SHOWN ONCE. The server stores only its hash and returns the
 * full link in the one response that generated it; this panel keeps it in
 * component state until the operator says they have it, and never writes it
 * to the query cache.
 *
 * INVARIANT 6: rendering this panel is a courtesy to a holder of
 * `enrollment.link.manage`. Every route checks the permission itself.
 */

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { ConfirmDialog } from "../../components/confirm";
import { describeSubmitError } from "../../components/form";
import { Field, inputClass } from "../../components/ui";
import type { InstallLinkSaved, InstallLinkSettings, InstallLinkUpdate } from "../../lib/api";
import { useApi } from "../../lib/ApiContext";
import { formatDateTime } from "../../lib/datetime";
import { Btn, Panel, QueryState, Toggle } from "../../shell/components";

export const INSTALL_LINK_KEY = ["install-link"] as const;
export const LINK_MANAGE = "enrollment.link.manage";

/** One network per line (or comma), blanks dropped. The server validates. */
export function parseNetworks(text: string): string[] {
  return text
    .split(/\r?\n|,/)
    .map((item) => item.trim())
    .filter((item) => item !== "");
}

/** ISO -> `<input type="datetime-local">`, in the browser's own time zone. */
export function toLocalInput(iso: string | null): string {
  if (!iso) return "";
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
}

/** `<input type="datetime-local">` -> ISO, or null for "no switch-off". */
export function fromLocalInput(value: string): string | null {
  return value ? new Date(value).toISOString() : null;
}

interface Draft {
  enabled: boolean;
  networks: string;
  disableAt: string;
  hourlyLimit: string;
}

function draftFrom(settings: InstallLinkSettings): Draft {
  return {
    enabled: settings.enabled,
    networks: settings.networks.join("\n"),
    disableAt: toLocalInput(settings.disable_at),
    hourlyLimit: String(settings.hourly_limit),
  };
}

function bodyFrom(draft: Draft): InstallLinkUpdate {
  return {
    enabled: draft.enabled,
    networks: parseNetworks(draft.networks),
    disable_at: fromLocalInput(draft.disableAt),
    hourly_limit: Number(draft.hourlyLimit),
  };
}

/** The settings without the link, which is what the cache may hold. */
function settingsOf(saved: InstallLinkSaved): InstallLinkSettings {
  return {
    enabled: saved.enabled,
    has_code: saved.has_code,
    networks: saved.networks,
    disable_at: saved.disable_at,
    hourly_limit: saved.hourly_limit,
    enabled_at: saved.enabled_at,
    joined_since_enabled: saved.joined_since_enabled,
  };
}

function statusLine(settings: InstallLinkSettings, zone?: string | null): string {
  if (!settings.enabled) {
    return settings.has_code
      ? "Off. Computers cannot join through the link."
      : "Off. No link has been generated yet.";
  }
  const joined = settings.joined_since_enabled;
  const parts = [
    `On since ${formatDateTime(settings.enabled_at, zone)}`,
    `${joined} ${joined === 1 ? "computer" : "computers"} joined since then`,
  ];
  if (settings.disable_at) {
    parts.push(`switches itself off ${formatDateTime(settings.disable_at, zone)}`);
  }
  return `${parts.join(" · ")}.`;
}

export function InstallLinkPanel({ zone }: { zone?: string | null }) {
  const api = useApi();
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const link = useQuery({ queryKey: INSTALL_LINK_KEY, queryFn: () => api.getInstallLink() });
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Draft | null>(null);
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<"idle" | "done" | "failed">("idle");
  const [confirmRegenerate, setConfirmRegenerate] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (link.data && draft === null) setDraft(draftFrom(link.data));
  }, [link.data, draft]);

  const onSaved = (saved: InstallLinkSaved) => {
    client.setQueryData(INSTALL_LINK_KEY, settingsOf(saved));
    setDraft(draftFrom(saved));
    if (saved.url) {
      setRevealed(saved.url);
      setCopied("idle");
    }
  };
  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: InstallLinkUpdate) => api.saveInstallLink(body),
    onSuccess: onSaved,
  });
  const regenerate = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.regenerateInstallLink(),
    onSuccess: onSaved,
  });

  const noNetwork = draft !== null && draft.enabled && parseNetworks(draft.networks).length === 0;
  const failure = save.error ?? regenerate.error;

  async function copy(text: string) {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied("done");
    } catch {
      setCopied("failed");
    }
  }

  return (
    <Panel
      title="Install link"
      subtitle="One link for every computer your technicians set up. Computers that join wait for review before they receive anything but inventory and health monitoring."
    >
      <QueryState
        loading={link.isLoading}
        error={link.error}
        empty={false}
        emptyLabel="The install link has no settings yet."
      >
        {draft && link.data ? (
          <form
            onSubmit={(event) => {
              event.preventDefault();
              save.mutate(bodyFrom(draft));
            }}
          >
            <p className="note">{statusLine(link.data, zone)}</p>
            {link.data.has_code && !revealed ? (
              <p className="note">
                The link was shown once, when it was generated. To see a link again, generate a new one.
              </p>
            ) : null}
            <Toggle
              checked={draft.enabled}
              onChange={(enabled) => setDraft({ ...draft, enabled })}
              label="Accept new computers through the link"
            />
            <Field
              label="Networks the link answers"
              help="One network per line, for example 10.20.0.0/16. From anywhere else the link looks like a page that does not exist."
            >
              <textarea
                className={inputClass}
                rows={4}
                value={draft.networks}
                onChange={(event) => setDraft({ ...draft, networks: event.target.value })}
              />
            </Field>
            <Field
              label="Switch off automatically on"
              help="Optional, in this computer's time zone. The link stops answering at that moment."
            >
              <input
                type="datetime-local"
                className={inputClass}
                value={draft.disableAt}
                onChange={(event) => setDraft({ ...draft, disableAt: event.target.value })}
              />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Most computers per hour" help="Between 1 and 500. Beyond it, the link asks the next computer to try later.">
              <input
                type="number"
                min={1}
                max={500}
                className={inputClass}
                value={draft.hourlyLimit}
                onChange={(event) => setDraft({ ...draft, hourlyLimit: event.target.value })}
              />
            </Field>
            {noNetwork ? (
              <p role="alert" className="form-error">
                List at least one network before switching the link on.
              </p>
            ) : null}
            {failure ? (
              <p role="alert" className="form-error">
                The install link was not changed. {describeSubmitError(failure)}
              </p>
            ) : null}
            <div className="form-actions">
              <Btn
                type="submit"
                variant="primary"
                label="Save install link"
                disabled={save.isPending || noNetwork}
              />
              {link.data.has_code ? (
                <Btn
                  label="Generate a new link…"
                  onClick={() => setConfirmRegenerate(true)}
                  disabled={regenerate.isPending}
                />
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </form>
        ) : null}
      </QueryState>

      {revealed ? (
        <section className="reveal" role="region" aria-label="The new install link">
          <p>
            <strong>Copy this link now.</strong> It is shown once; afterwards only a new link can be shown.
            Open it on the computer you are setting up.
          </p>
          <pre>{revealed}</pre>
          <div className="form-actions">
            <Btn label={copied === "done" ? "Copied" : "Copy link"} onClick={() => void copy(revealed)} />
            <Btn label="I have copied it" onClick={() => setRevealed(null)} />
          </div>
          {copied === "failed" ? (
            <p role="alert" className="form-error">The browser refused to copy. Select the link and copy it by hand.</p>
          ) : null}
        </section>
      ) : null}

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={confirmRegenerate}
        title="Generate a new install link"
        subject="The install link"
        body="The current link stops working at once. Computers already installed are not affected; anyone holding the old link can no longer use it."
        confirmLabel="Generate new link"
        destructive
        onConfirm={() => {
          setConfirmRegenerate(false);
          regenerate.mutate();
        }}
        onCancel={() => setConfirmRegenerate(false)}
      />
    </Panel>
  );
}
```

```ts
/**
 * The install link. MUTABLE, so switching it off and on, and regenerating, can
 * be walked in fixture mode. The code below is 32 characters of the real
 * alphabet and enrols nothing anywhere.
 */
const FIXTURE_LINK = "https://console.toeshee.example/i/FIXTURExNOTxAxREALxCODEx00000000";
let LINK: InstallLinkSettings = {
  enabled: true,
  has_code: true,
  networks: ["10.20.0.0/16", "10.30.0.0/16"],
  disable_at: at(5 * 24),
  hourly_limit: 50,
  enabled_at: at(-26),
  joined_since_enabled: 4,
};

const getInstallLink: FixtureMap["getInstallLink"] = () => LINK;

const saveInstallLink: FixtureMap["saveInstallLink"] = (body) => {
  if (body.enabled && body.networks.length === 0) {
    throw new ApiError(422, JSON.stringify({
      detail: "at least one network is required to switch the link on; an empty list would not mean 'anywhere'",
    }));
  }
  const minted = body.enabled && !LINK.has_code;
  LINK = {
    ...LINK,
    ...body,
    has_code: LINK.has_code || body.enabled,
    enabled_at: body.enabled ? (LINK.enabled ? LINK.enabled_at : at(0)) : null,
    joined_since_enabled: body.enabled && LINK.enabled ? LINK.joined_since_enabled : 0,
  };
  return { ...LINK, url: minted ? FIXTURE_LINK : null };
};

const regenerateInstallLink: FixtureMap["regenerateInstallLink"] = () => {
  LINK = { ...LINK, has_code: true };
  return { ...LINK, url: FIXTURE_LINK };
};
```
